### [206] [Finding Where the Buck Stops: An Automated Failure Attribution-Based Reflection Framework for Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28264)
*Xiaoqing Wang,Keman Huang,Bin Liang,Hongyu Li,Xiaoyong Du,Wuqiong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoCtOR是一个针对多智能体系统的反思框架，通过自动化故障归因识别关键错误智能体，再利用反事实推理生成修正后的关键错误步骤，仅让关键错误智能体进行针对性反思，从而避免对正常执行任务的智能体造成记忆污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）虽然能处理复杂任务，但失败率较高。当前的自我反思方法要求所有智能体对失败进行反思，忽略了失败通常由特定智能体（即关键错误智能体）导致的事实。强制正常工作的智能体进行反思会污染其记忆，引入错误的见解。

Method: DoCtOR框架包含三个步骤：1) 通过自动化失败归因识别关键错误步骤和关键错误智能体；2) 采用反事实推理生成修正后的关键错误步骤；3) 仅让关键错误智能体参与针对性反思。与PPO强化学习相结合以增强多智能体协作。

Result: 在HotPotQA、ChartQAPro和Mind2Web数据集上，DoCtOR相比初始成功率分别提升了22%、26%和27%，优于Reflexion、Retroformer和COPPER等基线方法。此外，研究还证明了诊断-修正范式的泛化性，并在低资源场景下验证了聚焦于关键错误步骤之后的推理反思可获得与完整失败轨迹反思相当的质量。

Conclusion: DoCtOR通过精准定位并只让关键错误智能体进行反思，有效提升了多智能体系统的任务成功率，同时避免了正常智能体的记忆污染问题，为多智能体反思机制提供了更高效、更精准的新范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by large language models have shown promise for complex tasks but suffer from high failure rates. Current self-reflection methods for MAS require all agents to reflect upon failure, overlooking a critical reality: failures typically stem from a specific agent leading the task astray, namely the decisive error agent, while others merely fulfill their regular duties. Forcing regular-behaving agents to reflect contaminates their memory with wrong insights. Hence, we propose DoCtOR (Diagnose-then-Correct PPO-enhanced Reflection), a novel reflection framework that enhances multi-agent collaboration. DoCtOR first identifies the decisive error step and decisive error agent through automated failure attribution, then employs counterfactual reasoning to generate a corrected decisive error step, and finally engages only the decisive error agent to produce targeted reflections. Experimental results show DoCtOR achieves 22%, 26%, and 27% improvements over initial success rates on HotPotQA, ChartQAPro, and Mind2Web datasets, outperforming Reflexion, Retroformer, and COPPER. We further establish the generalizability of our diagnose-then-correct paradigm and demonstrate that in low-resource settings, focusing reflection on reasoning steps after the decisive error step achieves comparable quality to reflecting on the complete failure trajectory.

</details>


### [207] [RECAST: Recent & Context-Aware Sampling for Test-Time Adaptation in Streaming Biosignals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28271)
*Yong-Yeon Jo,Junho Song,Joon-myoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECAST是一个轻量级采样模块，用于流式生物信号测试时自适应（TTA），通过结合时间新近性、上下文相似性和预测可靠性三个信号来选择最合适的样本进行模型更新，在血压数据集上显著提升精度和趋势追踪能力，且开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式生物信号存在个体差异和时间漂移问题，群体训练模型在长期监测中精度下降。虽然TTA能实现在线个性化，但关键问题——哪些样本应驱动模型更新——尚未解决。使用全部缓冲样本会使更新模糊，仅用最新段则噪声大且不稳定。

Method: 提出RECAST采样模块，构建每个适应批次时融合三个信号：时间新近性（temporal recency）、上下文相似性（contextual similarity）和预测可靠性（predictive reliability）。仅改变使用的样本，不修改模型架构或训练目标，可无缝集成到缓冲式TTA框架中。

Result: 在两个血压数据集上，RECAST相比基线和消融实验提升了估计精度和趋势追踪效果。两个数据集上的每位患者改善均具有统计显著性；常规基准上改善广泛，急诊科设置中增益集中在最困难患者上。每个片段仅增加亚秒级延迟（单GPU和CPU核）。

Conclusion: RECAST验证了在TTA中样本选择策略的重要性，通过"近期、对齐当前生理状态、可靠"的样本选择原则，在保持实用性的同时显著提升流式生物信号监测的个性化精度，为长期监测中的稳定模型适应提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Streaming biosignals vary across subjects and drift over time, so population-trained models lose accuracy during long-term monitoring. Test-time adaptation (TTA) enables online personalization by updating the model on incoming samples. But in a stream, a basic question is left open: \emph{which samples should drive each update?} Using all buffered samples blurs the update with irrelevant segments. Using only the latest segment makes the update noisy and unstable. The most useful samples are recent, aligned with the current physiological state, and reliable enough to learn from. We propose \textbf{RECAST} (REcent \& Context-Aware Sampling for TTA), a lightweight sampling module for buffered TTA frameworks. RECAST builds each adaptation batch from three signals: temporal recency, contextual similarity, and predictive reliability. It changes only which samples are used, leaving the model and the training objective unchanged. On two blood-pressure datasets, RECAST improves estimation accuracy and trend tracking over baselines and ablations. The per-patient gains are statistically significant on both datasets, with broad improvement on the regular benchmark and gains concentrated on the hardest patients in the emergency-department setting. RECAST stays practical, adding only sub-second latency per segment on a single GPU and CPU core.

</details>


### [208] [LoopArena: Benchmarking Models as Runtime Controllers for Loop Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28281)
*Yi Wang,Haopeng Zhang,Chengxiang Huang,Rui Dai,Kaikui Liu,Piotr Koniusz,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoopArena是一个用于评估"控制器"模型如何引导独立编码代理（Worker）完成长周期任务的基准测试。它提供三种互补的评测设置（Type I/II/III），分别覆盖不同执行范围与成本。最优严格成功率为24.69%，仍有很大提升空间，同时控制器间配对推理成本平均降低64.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环工程（Loop Engineering）正成为围绕编码代理组织开发工作的新实践，但即使拥有能力强大的编码代理，循环本身也可能因信任过时的进度记录、跳过必要的验证、错误分配预算或过早停止而失败。然而，一次端到端运行的最终结果无法区分成功或失败是源于循环的引导还是编码代理的执行能力。因此需要专门的基准来单独评估引导模型（Controller）的能力。

Method: LoopArena引入Controller-Worker架构：被评估的模型作为Controller，在每轮编码后接收结构化运行摘要，并指示一个固定的编码代理（Worker）下一步做什么或验证什么，或决定是否停止。基准包含三种互补设置：Type I通过执行验证的问题来评分下一步Loop Contract选择（评估时无需运行Worker）；Type II在完整任务选定片段上执行重复控制；Type III从原始状态评估成对完整任务。

Result: 完整任务上的最佳严格成功率为24.69%，表明长时程循环控制仍有大量改进空间。跨Controller对比中，配对推理成本平均降低64.4%，Type II在主要Core标准下与相关排序高度一致（Spearman's ρ=0.9747）。

Conclusion: LoopArena为评估循环工程中引导模型的能力提供了系统化基准，揭示了长时程循环控制的显著改进空间，同时展示了不同评测设置间的一致性，为后续循环工程研究奠定了评测基础。

Abstract: Loop Engineering is emerging as a practice for organizing development work around coding agents. Instead of writing each prompt by hand, practitioners design loops that monitor progress, assign work, run checks, and decide what the agent should do next. Even with a capable coding agent, a loop may trust a stale progress note, skip needed verification, spend its budget in the wrong direction, or stop before the task is safe to submit. Yet the final outcome of one end-to-end run cannot tell whether success or failure reflects the loop's guidance or the coding agent's ability to carry out the task. We introduce LoopArena, a benchmark for evaluating how well one model can guide a separate coding agent through a long-running task. The model under evaluation is the \textbf{Controller}: after each coding round, it receives a structured summary of the run and instructs a separate, fixed coding agent, the \textbf{Worker}, on what to do or verify next, or decides whether to stop. LoopArena evaluates this ability in three complementary settings that differ in execution scope and cost. Type I scores next-step Loop Contract selection through execution-validated questions without running the Worker at evaluation time. Type II executes repeated control over a selected slice of a full task, while Type III evaluates the paired full task from its original state. On full tasks, the best observed Strict Success Rate is \textbf{24.69\%}, leaving substantial room for improvement in long-horizon loop control. Across Controllers, the paired reduction in estimated inference cost averages \textbf{64.4\%}, and Type II produces a similar ordering under the main Core criterion (Spearman's \(ρ=\textbf{0.9747}\)). We release the benchmark data and evaluation code at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/LoopArena .

</details>


### [209] [Memristive-Friendly Hadamard Reservoir Computing: Structured, Multiplier-Free Recurrences at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28295)
*Andrea Ceni,Gianluca Milano,Carlo Ricciardi,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化正交算子（由符号对角、置换和快速Walsh-Hadamard变换构成）的储备池计算方案，替代传统稠密递归矩阵。该算子无需乘法器，参数O(N)且每步计算量O(NlogN)，在标准和忆阻友好的回声状态网络中均可实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储备池计算依赖固定递归层，适合神经形态硬件，但忆阻友好的储备池仍依赖稠密递归矩阵，物理实现成本高昂，阻碍了硬件部署。

Method: 用结构化正交算子替代稠密矩阵，算子由符号对角线、置换和快速Walsh-Hadamard变换组成，具有免乘法、O(N)参数和O(NlogN)运算的特点。数学分析证明其正交性可导出紧致的回声状态条件和可预测的噪声响应，并能在单次应用中混合整个状态。

Result: 在20个分类和7个回归基准上（储备池规模达N=8192），结构化模型匹配稠密正交储备池的性能，并比循环储备池平均表现更好，优势随规模扩大而增加。在三种硬件平台上，递归步比稠密乘积快50倍，内存小10^4倍。

Conclusion: 结构化正交储备池在保持性能的同时大幅降低硬件实现成本，对噪声、量化、器件失配和离散故障具有鲁棒性，是神经形态硬件上可行的储备池计算方案。

Abstract: Reservoir Computing (RC) designs Recurrent Neural Networks around a fixed, i.e., untrained, recurrent layer, and is a natural candidate for neuromorphic hardware. Memristive-friendly reservoirs derive the neuron dynamics from memristive-device kinetics, but still rely on dense recurrent matrices, which are expensive to realize physically. In this paper, we replace the dense matrix with a structured orthogonal operator, built from sign diagonals, a permutation, and a fast Walsh-Hadamard transform. The operator is multiplier-free, requires $O(N)$ parameters and $O(N\log N)$ operations per step, and is never materialized as a matrix. We instantiate it in a standard and in a memristive-friendly Echo State Network, with one binary input connection per unit.
  Our mathematical analysis shows that exact orthogonality yields an echo state condition that is tight in the recurrent scaling, and a noise response that is predictable at design time. Moreover, the operator mixes the whole state in a single application. Experiments on twenty classification and seven regression benchmarks, at reservoir sizes up to $N = 8192$, show that the structured models match dense orthogonal reservoirs, and achieve better mean performance than the cycle reservoir by a margin that widens with size. Furthermore, we time the recurrent step on three hardware platforms, where it is up to $50\times$ faster than a dense product and $10^4\times$ smaller in memory. Finally, we ablate the operator and measure the response to noise, quantization, device mismatch and discrete faults.

</details>


### [210] [MAIL: Memory-driven, Adaptive, Incremental, and Literature-grounded Framework for Hypothesis Generation in Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28315)
*Mahdi Babaei,Xueshen Li,Yutao Kuang,Jolene P. Reid,Yu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MAIL（Memory-augmented, Adaptive, Incremental, and Literature-grounded）的自动化框架，用于化学领域的假设生成。该框架将假设生成建模为时间上扎根的、由记忆驱动的推理过程，通过持续积累和重新解读先验知识来形成不断演变的概念路径，从而生成创新且化学上合理的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学文献量的爆炸式增长为生成新颖且有影响力的假设提供了前所未有的机会，但瓶颈在于如何高效地导航这一庞大的知识库。现有方法依赖静态灵感语料库、预定义启发式规则或劳动密集的人机交互流程，限制了可扩展性和新颖性。

Method: MAIL框架将假设生成形式化为一个时间上扎根的、由记忆驱动的推理过程。与依赖静态语料和预定启发式不同，MAIL通过不断积累和重新解读先验知识，让假设从持续演化的概念路径中涌现，实现了自适应、增量的文献基于的推理。

Result: 在公开的TOMATO-Chem数据集和新构建的HN-NS（nature/science高新颖性挑战）数据集上，MAIL生成的假设具有结构连贯性和机制合理性，在MIOS和MPOS指标上取得最高分数（更有效地恢复了历史目标假设的核心思想和方法要素），并获得最高的专家评价科学质量分数。

Conclusion: 结果表明LLM能够自主探索化学领域并生成既创新又化学上可行的假设，证明了自动化记忆增强框架在科学发现中的潜力。

Abstract: The ever-expanding volume of the chemical literature offers unprecedented opportunities to generate novel and impactful hypotheses. However, the bottleneck lies in efficiently navigating this vast knowledge base to formulate high-quality, experimentally meaningful insights.
  While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for this task, existing methods often rely on static inspiration corpora, predefined heuristics, or laborious human-in-the-loop pipelines and decision-support frameworks that limit scalability and novelty. In this work, we propose an automated approach, a Memory-augmented, Adaptive, Incremental, and Literature-grounded (MAIL) framework for hypothesis generation in chemistry. Our MAIL method formulates hypothesis generation as a temporally grounded, memory-driven reasoning process, where hypotheses emerge from an evolving conceptual path that continuously accumulates and reinterprets prior knowledge. We evaluated the MAIL framework on a public TOMATO-Chem dataset and a newly curated and disseminated high-novelty nature/science challenge (HN-NS) dataset. Across both datasets, MAIL generates structurally coherent and mechanistically plausible hypotheses, achieves the highest MIOS and MPOS by more effectively recovering the central ideas and methodological elements of the historical target hypotheses, and obtains the highest overall expert-evaluation scores for scientific quality. These results demonstrate the potential of LLMs to autonomously explore chemical domains and generate hypotheses that are both innovative and chemically plausible.

</details>


### [211] [Real-Valued Hyperdimensional Sequence Representations with Hadamard Product Binding and Shift Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28334)
*Kenny Schlegel,Dmitri A. Rachkovskij,Denis Kleyko,Amy Loutfi,Stefan Streif,Evgeny Osipov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对超维计算中分数幂编码仅支持循环卷积或复数乘法绑定、与哈达玛积不兼容的问题，提出了三种基于随机傅里叶特征的实值位置编码变体（逆傅里叶基线、正弦和仅余弦表示）。其中正弦变体提供显式代数移位算子，可直接对编码序列施加时间移位而无需重新编码。实验表明这些实值表示在时间序列分类上性能与标准分数幂编码相当，同时支持计算高效的哈达玛积绑定，正弦变体在实值实现与精确移位等变之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数幂编码（FPE）虽能提供相似性保持的位置向量并支持移位等变变换，但标准公式主要针对循环卷积或复数乘法等绑定操作设计，与实值向量的哈达玛积绑定不兼容，限制了其在超维计算中的适用范围。

Method: 从随机傅里叶特征出发设计实值位置编码，提出三种变体：(1)基于逆傅里叶变换的实值基线；(2)由随机傅里叶特征衍生的正弦表示（含显式代数移位算子）；(3)仅余弦表示。正弦变体允许直接对向量编码的序列表示施加时间移位。

Result: 在时间序列分类数据集上的实验表明，所提出的实值表示在分类性能上与标准分数幂编码相当，同时支持更高效的哈达玛积绑定计算。正弦变体综合表现最优，兼具高效的实值实现和精确的移位等变变换能力。

Conclusion: 本文成功弥合了分数幂编码与哈达玛积绑定之间的鸿沟，为超维计算中的实值序列表示提供了一套可行方案，其中正弦变体在效率与等变性质之间提供了最佳权衡。

Abstract: Encoding temporal order is a fundamental requirement for sequence representations in Hyperdimensional Computing. Fractional Power Encoding provides similarity-preserving position vectors whose inner products approximate shift-invariant kernels, and it supports shift-equivariant transformations of encoded sequence representations. However, standard formulations of Fractional Power Encoding are primarily designed for binding operations such as circular convolution or complex-valued multiplication, which limits their compatibility with Hadamard product binding of real-valued vectors. This paper develops real-valued position encodings motivated by Random Fourier Features, aiming to retain the desirable properties of Fractional Power Encoding while supporting Hadamard-based operations. We propose three real-valued position-encoding variants: a real-valued baseline based on the inverse Fourier transform, and Sinusoid and Cosine-only representations derived from Random Fourier Features. Among them, the Sinusoid variant provides an explicit algebraic shift operator, allowing temporal shifts to be applied directly to the vector-encoded sequence representation without re-encoding the shifted sequence. Experiments on time-series classification datasets show that the proposed real-valued representations achieve performance comparable to standard Fractional Power Encoding while enabling computationally efficient Hadamard product binding. The Sinusoid variant offers the most favorable trade-off, combining efficient real-valued implementation with exact shift-equivariant transformations.

</details>


### [212] [AGENT-O: A Semantic Agent Card Framework for Interoperable and Governed Healthcare AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28345)
*Pengze Li,Cui Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGENT-O是一个模块化本体框架，用于语义化表示健康导向的AI代理系统（Agent Card），并支持评估科学出版物中报告完整性。通过对279篇论文的五维度评估，发现运行时/架构（84.6%）、治理/安全（82.8%）和可复现性（78.1%）的报告缺口最大，而评估（25.8%）和基准流程对齐（29.8%）报告较完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康领域AI代理系统的报告缺乏标准化结构，导致关键信息（如运行时架构、治理、可复现性）在科学出版物中经常缺失，难以评估和比较不同系统的完整性与可靠性。

Method: 构建OWL 2/RDF本体AGENT-O，覆盖运行时、模型、工作流、工具、临床使用、评估、溯源性、治理和报告评估等维度；采用本体清单、OWL-RL推理、三套SHACL验证套件、12个SPARQL能力查询、三个案例以及模型辅助的报告完整性评估（279篇论文、五个维度）进行验证。

Result: 本体包含1,962个RDF三元组和1,922个Protege公理，252个活动类、198个活动对象属性和51个数据类型属性。所有SHACL套件在示例图上符合要求，所有能力查询返回预期证据，279篇论文全部完成评分。发现评估-规范缺口：评估和基准流程报告较一致，但运行时架构、治理和可复现性报告严重不足。

Conclusion: AGENT-O提供了可复用的本体、语义Agent Card配置文件和报告完整性工作流，支持结构化报告和缺口识别，但不评估代理质量或部署就绪度。

Abstract: AGENT-O is a modular ontology framework that defines a semantic Agent Card for representing health-oriented AI agent systems and supports assessment of reporting completeness in scientific publications. AGENT-O was developed as an OWL 2/RDF ontology covering runtime, models, workflow, tools, clinical use, evaluation, provenance, governance, and reporting assessment. Evaluation included ontology inventory, OWL-RL reasoning, three SHACL suites, 12 SPARQL competency queries, three cases, and model-assisted reporting-completeness assessment of 279 papers across five dimensions. The ontology contained 1,962 RDF triples and 1,922 Protege axioms, with 252 active classes, 198 active object properties, and 51 datatype properties. All SHACL suites conformed on example graphs, all competency queries returned prespecified evidence, and all 279 papers were scored. Incomplete reporting was highest for runtime/architecture (84.6%), governance/safety (82.8%), and provenance/reproducibility (78.1%), compared with evaluation (25.8%) and benchmark-process alignment (29.8%). AGENT-O supported semantic Agent Card representation and reporting assessment while revealing an evaluation-specification gap: evaluation and benchmark procedures were reported more consistently than runtime architecture, governance, and reproducibility. AGENT-O provides a reusable ontology, semantic Agent Card profile, and reporting-completeness workflow for structured reporting and gap identification, but does not assess agent quality or deployment readiness.

</details>


### [213] [Propagating construction-time knowledge quality into medical question answering: A framework grounded in clinical guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28360)
*Jie Hu,Junjie Wang,Shan Lu,Yifang Hu,Gong Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个质量感知框架，将构建临床指南知识图谱时的三元组质量（结构符合度SchemaConf与证据支持度EvidScore）融合为持久信号Q(t)，并传播到检索和证据提示环节，从而弥合KG构建质量与推理时证据使用之间的断层。实验表明该信号在跨版本/跨模型场景下判别力更强，显著降低QA中的知识遗漏和冲突输出，临床医生评分接近理想状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型从临床指南构建知识图谱时，抽取的三元组在结构有效性和证据支持程度上参差不齐；而图增强问答系统在检索时通常只优化查询相关性，很少复用构建阶段产生的质量信息。这导致构建期质量控制与推理期证据使用之间存在脱节，质量信号没有被充分利用。

Method: 提出质量感知框架，包含：(1) 将结构符合度(SchemaConf)与证据支持度(EvidScore)作为互补维度建模，融合为逐三元组质量信号Q(t)；(2) 不是仅用质量做过滤，而是将Q(t)及质量层级作为图属性保留，并传播到质量加权子图检索和层级条件化证据提示中；(3) 同时保留段落级来源溯源信息。

Result: 在中文糖尿病临床指南实验中：融合的Q(t)在三元组质量判别上优于单一指标（AUC 0.748 vs 0.703/0.645）；在指南问答中，检测所需知识遗漏从16.3%降至5.3%，冲突输出从16.3%降至2.7%，证据支撑精确率81.6%，无效引用接近零；临床医生盲评中完整框架4.68分优于无检索的4.21分，接近理想条件4.80分；跨生成器实验趋势一致。

Conclusion: 构建期三元组质量可以作为下游证据选择与呈现的持久信号，质量信号的效用具有分布依赖性。将构建期质量控制传播到推理阶段能显著改善临床指南问答的证据质量，且该框架在不同模型生成器下表现一致，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models have facilitated knowledge graph (KG) construction from clinical guidelines, but extracted triples vary in structural validity and evidential support. Meanwhile, graph-augmented question answering (QA) systems typically optimize query relevance during retrieval, with limited reuse of quality information produced during KG construction. This creates a disconnect between construction-time quality control and inference-time evidence use. We investigate whether construction-time triple quality can serve as a persistent signal for downstream evidence selection and presentation. We propose a quality-aware framework that models structural conformance (SchemaConf) and evidential support (EvidScore) as complementary dimensions and fuses them into a per-triple quality signal, Q(t). Rather than using quality solely for filtering, the framework retains Q(t) and derived quality tiers as graph attributes and propagates them into quality-weighted subgraph retrieval and tier-conditioned evidence prompting, while preserving passage-level provenance. Experiments on Chinese diabetes clinical guidelines show that the utility of the quality signal is distribution dependent. Under cross-version and cross-model shift, the fused Q(t) provides stronger triple-quality discrimination than either component alone (AUC 0.748 vs. 0.703 for EvidScore and 0.645 for SchemaConf). In guideline-grounded QA, propagating construction-time quality reduces required-knowledge omission from 16.3% to 5.3% and conflicting outputs from 16.3% to 2.7%, with an evidence-grounded precision of 81.6% and near-zero invalid citations. Blinded clinician ratings favor the full framework over no retrieval (4.68 vs. 4.21 on a five-point scale) and approach the oracle condition (4.80), while cross-generator experiments show consistent trends.

</details>


### [214] [GRACE:Gradient-guided Coreset Selection for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28361)
*Praveen Bushipaka,Andrea D'Angelo,Lucia Passaro,Tommaso Cucinotta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE，一种基于梯度引导的核心集选择方法，用于在大语言模型机器遗忘中从异构语料库中自动构造遗忘集和保留集，无需预先指定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘方法通常假设遗忘集和保留集是预先指定的，但现实场景中往往只有少量不当行为的示例，需要从异构语料库中推断出遗忘集和保留集，这一数据选择问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: GRACE首先从种子示例计算遗忘方向，利用非负正交匹配追踪选择梯度近似该方向的紧凑遗忘核心集；然后通过投影去除遗忘方向，在剩余梯度空间中使用聚类正交匹配追踪选择保留示例以保证模型效用。

Result: 在两个目标域、两个模型家族和四种遗忘算法上，GRACE在保持可比遗忘质量的同时提升了模型效用，特别是在与先前的基于梯度的选择方法相比时具有一致的改进。

Conclusion: GRACE证明了自动数据选择在大语言模型机器遗忘中的有效性，为在不预先指定遗忘/保留集的情况下实现高效遗忘提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning methods for Large Language Models typically assume pre-specified forget and retain sets. In realistic settings, however, requests may provide only a few examples of undesired behavior, requiring forget and retain sets to be inferred from heterogeneous corpora. We study this data-selection problem and propose GRACE , a gradient-guided coreset selection method that constructs both forget and retain sets for LLM unlearning. GRACE first computes a forget direction from seed examples that elicit the undesired behavior, then selects a compact forget coreset whose gradients approximate this direction using non-negative orthogonal matching pursuit. To preserve model utility, it selects retain examples after projecting out the forget direction and applying clustered orthogonal matching pursuit in the remaining gradient space. Across two target domains, two model families, and four unlearning algorithms, GRACE improves model utility while maintaining comparable forget quality, with particularly consistent gains over prior gradient-based selection methods.

</details>


### [215] [EvoUndo: Recoverability-Constrained Self-Evolution for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28363)
*Tanmay Sah,Dolly Sah,Harshul Jain,Tanya Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoUndo框架，用于表示、合成、诊断和独立验证LLM智能体运行时自我修改的可恢复性。在600个未见过的单次自我进化任务中，发现197个能力提升但无法通过可恢复性验证的突变。通过扩展恢复演算和精确状态寻址干预，将恢复成功率从0/197提升到最高191/197，证明可靠的智能体自我进化需要协同设计验证、状态接地、见证语义和恢复语言表达力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在运行时越来越多地修改自身提示、工具、中间件、资源和执行框架。虽然这种自我进化可以提升能力，但成功的突变可能留下持久影响，在不同状态下无法安全地逆转。现有方法依赖迭代提示，未能系统处理这些状态相关的可恢复性问题。

Method: 提出EvoUndo框架，包含：(1) 表示模型生成的自我修改及其恢复表示；(2) 合成并诊断跨反事实状态的自我修改；(3) 独立验证可恢复性。采用确定性预言机分析，通过扩展恢复演算（从L0到更丰富的语言）和协议锁定的2x2接地-表达力干预来分离两个瓶颈：精确状态地址接地和恢复语言表达力。

Result: 在600个任务中发现197个可恢复性验证失败。原始恢复表示下传统修复策略恢复0/197；确定性预言机在L0下恢复48/197，扩展恢复演算将预言机恢复提升至191/197。精确状态地址接地将恢复率从0/48提升至38/48（79.2%），扩展语言在S1层实现142/143（99.3%）恢复。在gpt-oss-120b骨干上，添加精确地址诊断到更丰富语言使恢复降至133/143（93.0%），Qwen3.8-27B复制未呈现此负交互，表明该效应依赖模型。

Conclusion: 可靠的智能体自我进化不能仅依赖迭代提示，需要协同设计验证机制、状态接地、见证语义和恢复语言表达力。研究还揭示模型间存在依赖差异，单一模型的结果可能无法泛化。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly modify their own prompts, tools, middleware, resources, and execution harnesses at runtime. Such self-evolution can improve capability, but a successful mutation may leave persistent effects that cannot be safely reversed in states different from the one in which it was created. We introduce EvoUndo, a framework for representing, synthesizing, diagnosing, and independently verifying recoverability of model-generated self-modifications across counterfactual states. Across 600 unseen one-shot self-evolution tasks, we identify 197 capability-improving mutations that fail recoverability verification. Under the original recovery representation, conventional repair strategies recover 0/197 of these natural failures. Deterministic oracle analysis recovers 48/197 under the original recovery language L0, while the extended recovery calculus increases empirical oracle recovery to 191/197. A protocol-locked 2x2 grounding-by-expressivity intervention then separates two bottlenecks: exact state-address grounding increases successful recovery from 0/48 to 38/48 (79.2%) when the original language is sufficient, while extending the recovery language enables recovery on 142/143 (99.3%) failures in the oracle-defined S1 stratum. On the primary gpt-oss-120b backbone, adding exact-address diagnostics to the richer language reduces recovery to 133/143 (93.0%); a Qwen3.8-27B replication preserves the grounding and expressivity effects but not this negative interaction, indicating that the latter is model-dependent. These results indicate that reliable agent self-evolution requires co-designing verification, state grounding, witness semantics, and recovery-language expressivity rather than relying on iterative prompting alone.

</details>


### [216] [MAP: A Benchmark on Multimodal Accessibility Planning for Real World Places](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28384)
*Jason Armitage,Ioannis Tsochantaridis,Linda Mazzone,Chuqiao Yan,Srini Narayanan,Sarah Ebling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MAP，这是第一个用于评估多模态AI系统作为有可访问性需求用户的助手（在规划实地访问时）的基准测试。MAP包含两项新评估：可访问性规划的声明验证和可访问性规划的视觉证据检索，并支持随时间刷新数据以应对信息变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中存在大量有可访问性需求的用户（如行动不便、视障等），他们在规划出行时需要AI助手提供可访问性相关的信息支持。然而，现有的多模态AI评估基准缺乏针对这一特定场景的评测，无法衡量系统是否能够可靠地验证或推荐满足可访问性需求的场所。

Method: 构建MAP基准，包含两项新颖评估任务：(1) 可访问性规划的声明验证——评估系统能否验证关于场所及其可访问性特征的声明，并识别满足可访问性要求的场所；(2) 可访问性规划的视觉证据检索——评估多模态AI系统能否为指定场所和可访问性特征选择相应的视觉证据。评测采用自动评分和部分人工评分相结合的方式，并设计了一套支持在计划时间点刷新基准数据和真实标签的方法论，以应对场所信息和可访问性信息随时间变化的问题。

Result: 本文主要贡献在于提出了MAP这一新颖基准，填补了多模态AI在可访问性规划场景评估的空白，并设计了支持时间变化数据刷新的评估方法论。

Conclusion: MAP为评估多模态AI系统在辅助有可访问性需求的用户规划实地访问方面的能力提供了首个标准化基准，其自动与人工结合的评分机制以及随时间刷新的数据设计，为未来该领域的研究和系统改进奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce MAP, the first benchmark to evaluate multimodal AI systems as assistants for users with accessibility requirements when planning visits to places in the real world. In our evaluation, systems are presented with requests to verify or recommend a point of interest meeting an accessibility requirement. MAP contains two novel assessments: Claim verification for accessibility planning assesses if information on places and stated accessibility features is supported and identifies places that satisfy requested accessibility features. Visual evidence retrieval for accessibility planning checks if a multimodal AI system can select visual evidence for the requested place and accessibility feature. Our methodology supports comparison of AI systems in a setting where place information and accessibility information can change over time by evaluating systems and refreshing ground truth data at scheduled times. The benchmark is based on automatic rating and human rating for a proportion of responses.

</details>


### [217] [Timing-Aware Repurchase Prediction for Web-Scale E-Commerce: Survival Models for Multi-Surface Grocery Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28393)
*Akshay Kekuda,Shreeranjani Srirangamsridharan,Ishan Bhatt,Yanan Cao,Sinduja Subramaniam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kaushiki Nag,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用生存模型（AFT）替代传统的二分类回购预测模型，直接预测回购时间。在多级电商平台上用超过30种消融配置评估，发现Log-Normal模型在边际拟合和排序上最优，而Exponential AFT在校准误差上最优（ECE~1e-4，比Log-Normal低约10倍）。单个AFT模型可替代三个不同时间窗口的二分类器，且使用约3倍少的树。最终采用Exponential AFT用于概率消耗场景、Log-Normal用于纯排序场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的电商回购推荐系统将问题框定为二分类："客户是否会在W天内购买该商品"，这需要为每个时间窗口单独训练模型。作者希望用生存模型直接预测回购时间，用一个模型替代多个二分类器，从而简化系统架构并提高效率。

Method: 使用加速失效时间（AFT）生存模型直接预测回购时间，在大型杂货电商平台上系统评估超过30种消融配置。对Log-Normal、Weibull、Exponential等AFT模型族进行边际风险分析、排序性能和校准质量对比；通过4参数参数化校准将原始生存CDF映射到各时间窗口的概率。

Result: 1) 经验风险分析显示递减的边际风险（k~0.9），与常识相反；Log-Normal边际拟合最优（R²=0.998）。2) 单个AFT模型替代三个二分类器，匹配或超越各时域性能，且树数量减少约3倍。3) Exponential AFT校准最优（ECE~1e-4），比Log-Normal低10倍，而排序指标差异仅0.3%。

Conclusion: 生存模型是电商回购预测的有效替代方案，能用一个模型覆盖所有时间窗口。在AFT家族内部存在原理性的校准-排序权衡：Exponential AFT适合概率消耗场景，Log-Normal适合纯排序场景。该发现为非参数化、自动校准的生存模型研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Repurchase recommenders in e-commerce are commonly framed as a binary question asking "will this customer buy this item within W days", a formulation that requires a separately trained model for every horizon of interest. We replace this stack with survival models that predict time-to-repurchase directly, and evaluate them on millions of customers from a major grocery e-commerce platform across more than thirty ablation configurations. Our study makes three contributions. First, an empirical hazard analysis reveals a slightly decreasing marginal hazard (k ~ 0.9), differing from the common intuition that grocery items become more likely to be repurchased the longer since the last purchase (increasing hazard, k > 1). Log-Normal achieves the best marginal fit (R^2 = 0.998) and the best ranking, despite Weibull providing the best conditional residual fit, revealing an apparent discrepancy we analyze in detail. Second, a single Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model replaces three per-horizon binary classifiers, matching or exceeding each at its own horizon while using roughly 3x fewer total trees. Feature importance reshuffles under the survival objective: channel-cadence and recency signals rise while aggregate frequency counts fall. Third, a 4-parameter parametric calibration maps raw survival CDFs to per-horizon probabilities with zero cross-horizon monotonicity violations. Calibration quality varies by an order of magnitude across the AFT family: Exponential AFT (Weibull k=1) achieves expected calibration error (ECE) ~1e-4, roughly 10x lower than Log-Normal, while ranking metrics agree within 0.3% relative. We adopt Exponential AFT for probability-consuming surfaces and Log-Normal for pure ranking, exposing a principled calibration-ranking trade-off within a single AFT family.

</details>


### [218] [RetailAgent: Structured Adverse Timing in Self-Conditioned Multimodal LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28399)
*Yupeng Zhang,Liuyuan Jiang,Hongyi Huang,Bingheng Li,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过RetailAgent实验框架研究LLM代理在金融交易中的方向性结构，发现LLM在日内股票价格历史中做出多空决策时，存在持久的负向择时偏差——即"持有"间隔的收益显著低于"空仓"间隔。这种效应跨模态、跨时间跨度、跨状态和跨模型家族均稳定存在，且行为序列打乱后效应减弱，表明动作与后续收益之间的对齐驱动力了负向评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融市场中，对价格变动做出系统性反应的顺序策略可能被其他参与者预测。理解LLM代理是否展现出可预测的方向性结构，对于研究其行为特征以及他人如何对可预测策略做出反应具有重要意义。

Method: 构建RetailAgent实验框架：让LLM观察匿名的日内股票价格历史和允许状态，反复在下一间隔收益揭示之前选择做多（持有）或空仓（不参与）。采用暴露匹配度量，即比较同一股票日内路径上做多与空仓间隔的收益（移除做多决策的总体比例），并通过打乱行动序列、引入自撰记忆等方式进行消融分析。

Result: 暴露匹配度量揭示了跨模态、时间跨度、状态和模型家族持久的负向择时效应。打乱保存的行为序列显著减弱了该效应，表明行为与后续收益间的对齐驱动了负评分。引入自撰记忆会增加策略持续性，而在代理同时使用两种行为的股票日中，择时偏差更为负向。

Conclusion: LLM金融决策中存在稳定、可恢复的方向性结构，这为研究他人如何应对可预测策略提供了行为信号。

Abstract: In financial markets, a sequential policy that reacts systematically to price movements may become predictable to other market participants. This paper studies whether large language model (LLM) agents exhibit such directional structure through RetailAgent, an experimental framework in which an LLM observes anonymized intraday equity price histories and permitted state, then repeatedly chooses long (hold the stock) or flat (stay out) before the subsequent interval return is revealed. We compare returns during long and flat intervals along the same stock's intraday path after removing the overall fraction of long decisions. This exposure-matched measure reveals persistent negative timing across modality, horizon, state, and model family. Shuffling saved action sequences substantially attenuates the effect, showing that alignment between actions and subsequent returns drives the negative score. Feeding self-authored memories into decisions further increases policy persistence, while timing becomes more negative among stock-days on which the agent uses both actions. These results reveal stable, recoverable directional structure in sequential LLM financial decisions and a behavioral signal for studying how another participant could respond to a predictable policy.

</details>


### [219] [VERA-8B: Evidence-Grounded Audit Risk Reasoning from SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28402)
*Menghan Liu,Elynn Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VERA-8B，一个端到端的审计推理系统，用于在执行行动之前识别审计风险。该系统首次统一了SFT和GRPO方法，实现基于证据的审计推理，并引入弃权机制和不确定性限定来处理信息不完整的案例，同时设计了AuditBridge将原始文件转化为可验证记录和审阅报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准金融语言模型优先考虑流畅性而非证据性，仅适用于一般金融推理，可能产生看似合理但模糊的答案，存在"证据缺口"（grounding gap），使其不适合审计工作。审计工作必须基于合理证据进行判断，因此需要专门针对审计场景的推理系统。

Method: VERA-8B通过以下方式构建：(1) 首次统一SFT（监督微调）和GRPO（组相对策略优化）在统一证据标准下进行审计推理；(2) 引入弃权（abstention）和不确定性限定机制，以推迟不确定或证据不完整的案例；(3) 设计AuditBridge，将原始文件转换为已验证记录，再生成审阅者就绪的报告，桥接金融与计算。

Result: VERA-8B在性能上超越了所有评估的基线模型，证明了统一SFT和GRPO的证据驱动审计推理方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 这些组件共同产生了可审计、可供审阅的输出，适合实际审计工作。由于审计不能容忍无根据的论断，弃权机制和证据基础是该系统的重要设计特色。

Abstract: Across audit applications, judgments must be supported by reasonable evidence. However, standard financial language models prioritize fluency over evidence. They are built for general financial reasoning and may produce plausible but ambiguous answers, creating a grounding gap that makes them unsuitable for audit work. We address this gap with VERA-8B, a new end-to-end audit reasoning system that identifies audit risks before enforcement actions occur. Constructing such a model raises several challenges, as no prior machine learning work targets pre-enforcement audit prediction. To our knowledge, we are the first to unify SFT and GRPO for evidence-grounded audit reasoning under one evidence standard, achieving performance that surpasses all evaluated baselines. Because auditing cannot tolerate unsupported claims, we introduce abstention and uncertainty qualification to defer uncertain or evidence-incomplete cases. Finally, we design an AuditBridge to ground model reasoning for practical audit work. It transforms raw filings into verified records and then into reviewer-ready reports, bridging finance and computation with broad generality. Together, these components produce auditable, review-ready outputs suitable for practical audit work.

</details>


### [220] [Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards: Symbolic Backpropagation for Post-Training LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28421)
*Vishvesh Bhat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PLVR（带可验证奖励的程序学习）方法，将推理能力从模型权重中移出，以显式程序形式呈现。通过符号反向传播（在程序结构上进行类型推断式梯度分配）学习程序，在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B模型比同预算RL高出27.8分，比大一个数量级的前沿模型高出13.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后训练（SFT和RL）将推理能力编码进模型权重中，导致能力不可检查、不可逐步验证、不可迁移到其他模型。作者认为对于中间步骤可验证的任务，推理应放在模型权重之外，以显式程序形式存在。

Method: PLVR通过符号反向传播从输入输出样例中学习程序：每个程序层携带类型本体，在输出端计算损失后，通过原始函数签名的类型推断将所需输入本体反向传播，这是链式法则的类比（信用分配是推导而非估计）。与RLVR验证终端结果不同，PLVR的奖励是逐步契约判定，密集覆盖于程序结构之上。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B基础模型配PLVR比同预算RL平均高27.8分，比大一个数量级的前沿模型高13.6分。单一原始函数库可用于两个基准，新任务边际成本仅需100个程序搜索样例且无需新微调数据。消融实验表明，用均匀采样替代损失引导搜索会使中位程序从65.6降至17.5，证明优势来自反向传播而非类型系统。

Conclusion: 推理应作为显式程序放置于模型权重之外，PLVR通过符号反向传播实现可验证的程序学习。该方法具有可检查性、可迁移性和低成本扩展性，作者开源了符号反向传播库和一致性检查器以支持推广到其他原始函数库。

Abstract: Post training a language model to reason means updating its weights. Supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning both place the acquired capability inside the model where it cannot be inspected cannot be checked step by step and cannot be moved to another model. We argue that for tasks whose intermediate steps admit verification, reasoning is better placed outside the base models weights as an explicit program composed from deterministic and neural primitives. We introduce PLVR (Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards): a post training method that learns such programs directly from input-output examples. Its mechanism is symbolic backpropagation: each program layer carries a typed ontology a loss is computed at the output against ground truth and required input ontologies are propagated backward by type inference over primitive signatures: an analogue of the chain rule in which credit assignment is a derivation rather than an estimate. Where RLVR verifies a terminal outcome, PLVRs reward is a per step contract verdict dense over program structure. On LiveCodeBench v6 and Tau2Bench, 30B base models with PLVR outperform RL at matched budget by 27.8 points on average and frontier models an order of magnitude larger by 13.6 points. A single primitive library serves two benchmarks, so the marginal cost of a new task is 100 examples of program search and no new finetuning data. Replacing the loss guided search with uniform sampling over the same type admissible space at equal budget collapses the median program from 65.6 to 17.5, identifying the backward pass rather than the type system as the source of the advantage. We release the symbolic backpropagation library and a conformance checker so the method can be applied to primitive libraries other than our own.

</details>


### [221] [Prove2Me: An Open Collaborative Platform for Scaling Math Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28433)
*Shuze Chen,Kunal Marwaha,Xiaoyang Lu,Henry Yuen,Tianyi Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prove2Me是一个开放协作平台，利用AI智能体将数学形式化转变为可规模化、众包式的工作，任何人都可以通过智能体参与Lean 4形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大规模形式化数学项目面临高门槛：需要形式化验证专业知识、耗费大量时间。AI编码智能体虽降低了这些障碍，但缺乏一个支持人类与AI大规模协作、机器验证正确性的平台。

Method: Prove2Me平台允许用户启动形式化"任务"（missions），AI智能体贡献形式化证明。设计了专门的机制和工具链（harness），支持大规模协作，使智能体能够互相构建工作并自由复用已有结果。

Result: 提出了一个开放平台并已上线（prove2.me），旨在将数学形式化转变为可扩展的、众包的、任何拥有智能体的人都能参与的努力。

Conclusion: Prove2Me有望实现互联网规模的数学协作，结合人类与AI智能体，并通过机器检查确保正确性，从而开启形式化数学的新范式。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean 4 promise the paradigm of formally verified mathematics, but large-scale formalization projects have faced major barriers to entry, including the need for expertise in formal verification (as well as the underlying mathematics) and the significant time required for writing formal proofs. AI coding agents have dramatically reduced these barriers; human users can now use natural language to prompt agents to write complex proofs in Lean. This opens up the intriguing possibility of internet-scale mathematical collaboration involving both humans and AI agents, where correctness is machine-checked.
  To realize this possibility, we introduce Prove2Me (https://prove2.me), an open collaborative platform for formalizing mathematics. Users launch formalization "missions", to which AI agents contribute formal proofs toward completion. We designed mechanisms and a specialized harness in Prove2Me that enable large-scale collaboration so that agents can build on one another's work and freely reuse existing results. In doing so, Prove2Me aims to turn math formalization into a scalable, crowd-sourced effort open to anyone with an agent.

</details>


### [222] [Learning to Use Tools: Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28447)
*Minghui Xu,Zi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在Countdown数学推理任务中集成计算器工具调用的方法。通过分析发现计算错误占错误回答的很大比例，因此构建SFT数据集教授模型工具使用模式，并在此基础上应用多种在线强化学习方法（RLOO、RLOO++、GRPO、DAPO）。实验表明计算器工具集成使SFT和RL基线均提升约10个百分点，其中Tool-DAPO表现最佳，将pass@1从35.8%提升至66.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在数学推理任务中常因计算不准确而失败，特别是Countdown这类需要可靠计算和验证的任务。作者希望通过外部计算器工具集成来减少算术错误，提升数学推理的可靠性。

Method: 1) 分析推理失败模式，确认计算错误是主要问题；2) 构建SFT数据集教授工具调用模式和结果解释方法；3) 在工具格式化策略基础上应用多种on-policy RL方法（RLOO、RLOO++、GRPO、DAPO）；4) 使用自动可验证的最终答案奖励信号；5) 构建全新的1,024题held-out基准用于可靠评估。

Result: 计算器工具集成在SFT和RL基线中均带来一致的提升，pass@k提高约10个百分点。Tool-DAPO表现最佳，pass@1从Tool-SFT的35.8%提升至66.0%。进一步分析显示，即使仅提供最终答案奖励，RL也能鼓励更有效的工具使用。

Conclusion: 工具集成能显著减少计算和验证错误，而强化学习能提高正确推理轨迹的概率。两者结合为提升LLM数学推理能力提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Current large language models (LLMs) increasingly benefit from external tool integration, especially for tasks requiring reliable computation and verification. Motivated by this, we study calculator tool calling for improving mathematical reasoning on the Countdown task. We first analyze reasoning failures and find that calculation errors account for a substantial portion of incorrect responses. We then construct supervised fine-tuning datasets to teach the model useful tool-use patterns and how to interpret returned outputs. Building on this tool-formatted policy, we apply several on-policy reinforcement learning methods, including RLOO, RLOO++, GRPO, and DAPO, using automatically verifiable final-answer rewards. To enable a more reliable evaluation, we construct a fresh 1,024-problem held-out Countdown benchmark with no exact overlap with the training data. Our results show that calculator tool integration consistently improves both SFT and RL baselines, yielding roughly 10 percentage-point gains across pass@k. Among the RL methods, Tool-DAPO achieves the strongest performance, improving pass@1 from 35.8% for Tool-SFT to 66.0%. Further analysis shows that RL encourages more effective tool use even when only final-answer rewards are provided. These findings suggest that tool integration reduces arithmetic and verification errors, while RL increases the probability of correct reasoning traces.

</details>


### [223] [COVER: Identifiable Evaluation of Coalition Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28475)
*Raghul Sugumar,Amrit Gopinath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COVER评估契约方法，用于在多智能体系统中隔离和量化"路由效应"（即团队选择对结果的影响）。通过固定信息边界、下游栈G和有限合法团队族，COVER能在假设最少的情况下精确计算有限基准上的oracle遗憾（regret），从而在不制造虚假路由收益的前提下，暴露路由选择的空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在更换团队时，其消息和最终答案都会改变，因此端到端的准确率差距本身无法单独归因于路由效应。现有评估方法无法区分"路由选择带来的提升"与"其他因素带来的变化"，需要一种能够准确识别路由效应的评估契约。

Method: COVER方法的核心是评估契约：在执行结果生成之前，固定公共信息边界、下游栈G和有限合法团队族。完全覆盖（complete coverage）确保在给定栈条件下精确计算有限基准的oracle遗憾。对于任意有限策略集合，执行其各自不同所选团队的并集，是支持所有成对策略对比的最小无假设依据，但不支持绝对oracle遗憾。此外设计了两个受控表格（源ID不相交分割）来检验该工具，并进行了交叉栈诊断以检查G依赖性和路由与终结器的交互作用。

Result: 在MuSiQue-12上，预设的特权正对照将遗憾从0.532降至0.402；后续公共接口对照达到0.424（对比0.554）但属回顾性。在HotpotQA-4上，预设的公共直接评分器将遗憾从0.313降至0.110。在固定栈Llama执行中，验证的路由遗憾改善0.190，而原始答案增益仅0.010且置信区间跨零。五族ToolSandbox变体偏移验证中，声明族oracle达到0.768安全证据完成率，而前瞻性冻结路由器仅为0.637（遗憾0.131），未通过预设的0.10标准。交叉栈诊断显示绝对分数依赖G，但未发现路由与终结器之间的可检测交互。

Conclusion: COVER是一种可审计的测量方法论，而非关于栈不变性或通用智能体路由优越性的声明。它能够暴露路由选择余量（selection headroom），而不制造虚假的路由胜绩，为多智能体系统中的路由效应提供了严谨的评估框架。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system changes its team, it also changes the messages and final answer it produces, so an end-to-end accuracy gap does not by itself identify a routing effect. We introduce method, an evaluation contract that fixes a public information boundary, downstream stack G, and finite legal team family before outcomes are generated. Complete coverage identifies exact finite-benchmark oracle regret conditional on that stack. For any finite collection of frozen policies, executing the union of their distinct selected teams is the minimal assumption-free support for every pairwise policy contrast, though not for absolute oracle regret. Two controlled tables with source-ID-disjoint splits test the instrument. On MuSiQue-12, a pre-specified privileged positive control improves regret from 0.532 to 0.402; a later public-interface control reaches 0.424 versus 0.554 but is retrospective. On HotpotQA-4, a pre-specified public direct scorer improves regret from 0.313 to 0.110. In fixed-stack Llama execution, verified route regret improves by 0.190, while the raw-answer gain is 0.010 with an interval crossing zero. A five-family ToolSandbox variant-shift validation exhaustively evaluates 16 declared teams on 14 untouched task variants (224/224 valid rows): the declared-family oracle reaches 0.768 safe-evidence completion, while the prospectively frozen router gets 0.637 (regret 0.131), failing the predeclared 0.10 criterion. A later retrospective comparator reaches 0.655, matching all-workers with 4.57 versus 5.00 workers on average. Thus COVER exposes selection headroom without manufacturing a routing win. A crossed-stack diagnostic shows absolute scores depend on G but finds no detectable router-by-finalizer interaction. COVER is an auditable measurement methodology, not a claim of stack-invariant or universal agent-routing superiority.

</details>


### [224] [AcrossVAM1.0: Particle World Modeling for Text-Assisted Robot Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28491)
*Yafei Zhang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AcrossVAM1.0 是一个轻量级、文本辅助的视频动作模型，它将机器人视频预测分解为基于粒子的运动推理和基于最后一帧的外观生成，在自建 VRS 基准上将轨迹误差降低 21%，并将未来帧 PSNR/SSIM 从 19.97/0.796 提升至 20.573/0.8004，但 LPIPS 未超越持久性基线，语言接地效果也较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单体像素模型将运动推理和高频外观保留这两种目标纠缠在一起，且其进展往往被强大的最后一帧基线所掩盖，难以清晰评估模型的真实进步。

Method: 利用冻结的 SAM3-DLP 编解码器将四帧上下文分解为机器人、手臂、夹爪的语义粒子及背景潜变量；用 0.28M 参数的时空 Transformer 对齐粒子身份并前向滚动状态，通过冻结的 OpenCLIP 指令嵌入经 FiLM 调制；因果双流解码器将粒子渲染的运动与仅基于最后一帧的外观编码结合，通过残差精炼器和学习式传递掩码生成五帧未来画面。

Result: 在真实机器人轨迹构成的 VRS 基准上，粒子动力学使轨迹误差较持久性基线降低 21%；三组传递掩码种子下未来帧 PSNR/SSIM 从 19.97/0.796 提升至 20.573/0.8004；原始粒子生成使运动区域 PSNR 从 11.89 提升至 13.23。但 LPIPS 未超越持久性基线，语言条件改变仅带来 2.8–3.1% 的轨迹误差变化。

Conclusion: 显式粒子动力学作为机器人视频预测的低维接口具有前景，但鲁棒的语言接地和外观传递仍是主要未解决的挑战。作者同时报告了 oracle、阴性对照、多种子及逐机器人分析等局限性。

Abstract: Predicting robot videos requires both precise motion reasoning and preservation of high-frequency appearance, yet monolithic pixel models entangle these objectives and often conceal their progress behind a strong last-frame baseline. We present AcrossVAM1.0, a lightweight, text-assisted video action model that factorizes future prediction into object-centric motion and dense appearance. A frozen SAM3-DLP codec decomposes four context frames into semantic particles for the robot, arm, and gripper, together with a background latent. A 0.28M-parameter spatio-temporal Transformer aligns particle identities, rolls their states forward, and is modulated by a frozen OpenCLIP instruction embedding through FiLM. A causal dual-stream decoder combines particle-rendered motion with appearance encoded exclusively from the last observed frame; a residual refiner and learned delivery mask produce five future frames without access to future appearance. On our VRS benchmark constructed from diverse real-robot trajectories, particle dynamics reduce trajectory error by 21.0\% over persistence. Across three delivery-mask seeds, AcrossVAM1.0 improves future-frame PSNR/SSIM from 19.97/0.796 to 20.573/0.8004, while raw particle generation improves motion-region PSNR from 11.89 to 13.23. The delivered model does not yet beat persistence in LPIPS, and correct-versus- shuffled language changes trajectory error by only 2.8--3.1%. We report these limitations alongside oracle, negative-control, multi-seed, and per-robot analyses. The results show that explicit particle dynamics are a promising low-dimensional interface for robot video prediction, while robust language grounding and appearance delivery remain the principal open challenges.

</details>


### [225] [Training Communication-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Language Models with Layer Re-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28511)
*Simeng Sun,Roger Waleffe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通信高效的MoE模型（CE-MoE），通过异构层模式解耦token混合和通道混合深度，将专家容量集中在少数路由MoE层中，同时通过增加token混合层和密集FFN层来维持深度。在2B到31.5B参数规模上，CE-MoE在匹配验证损失和下游基准的同时，显著降低了训练成本（31.5B规模节省33.3% GPU小时），并提升了推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在训练MoE语言模型时，专家并行的all-to-all token调度与组合通信操作会消耗大量端到端训练时间，这是一个显著的效率瓶颈。

Method: 采用异构层模式，解耦token混合层与通道混合层（MoE层）的深度；将专家容量集中于少数路由MoE层，用额外的token混合层和密集FFN层维持模型深度，从而减少MoE层间的通信开销。

Result: 在2B到31.5B参数规模的缩放阶梯上，CE-MoE在匹配验证损失和下游基准性能的前提下持续降低训练成本。在31.5B规模下，节省33.3% GPU小时，同时提升平均下游分数和推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: CE-MoE证明了通过合理分配路由层密度，可以在不牺牲模型质量的前提下大幅削减MoE训练中的通信开销，是一种有效的通信高效MoE设计范式。

Abstract: When training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models with expert parallelism, all-to-all token dispatch and combine collectives can consume a substantial fraction of end-to-end training time. In this work, we study communication-efficient MoE models (CE-MoE), in which we adopt a heterogeneous layer pattern that decouples token-mixing and channel-mixing depth. Compared to conventional models which interleave MoE layers after each token-mixing layer (e.g., attention, Mamba-2), CE-MoE models concentrate expert capacity in a select few routed MoE layers, while maintaining depth by adding additional token-mixing and dense-FFN layers. Across a scaling ladder from 2B to 31.5B total parameters, under matched total and activated parameters, CE-MoE models consistently reduce training cost while matching validation loss and downstream benchmarks with full-MoE baselines. At the 31.5B scale, CE-MoE uses 33.3\% fewer GPU-hours while improving average downstream score and inference throughput.

</details>


### [226] [InstructMesh: Selective Refinement of Generative 3D Models for Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28534)
*Faraz Faruqi,Ahmed Katary,Demircan Tas,Theresa Hradilak,Ning Zhang,Jiaji Li,Fabian Manhardt,Martin Nisser,Vrushank Phadnis,Ruofei Du,Federico Tombari,Megan Hofmann,Stefanie Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InstructMesh是一个交互式后生成精修工具，允许用户通过区域选择和有针对性的操作（如开孔、密封空隙、调整局部厚度）来修复生成式3D模型的几何缺陷，直接操作中间潜在表示，无需专业建模技能，并通过两项用户研究验证了新手用户能够识别和执行与制造相关的修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式AI 3D建模工具优先考虑视觉逼真度而非几何精度，生成的模型常存在影响实际制造的缺陷，而现有方法缺乏针对性的修复手段。

Method: 分析了最先进生成工具输出中常见的与制造相关的失效模式；设计了InstructMesh工具，支持通过自然语言提示或滑块控件进行区域选择和定向编辑操作（如开孔、密封空隙、局部厚度调整），直接在中间潜在表示上操作以实现鲁棒的几何修正。

Result: 通过两项用户研究，证明新手用户能够使用InstructMesh识别并执行与制造相关的修复；研究还揭示了用户偏好混合界面（滑块控件结合自然语言输入）。

Conclusion: InstructMesh为生成式3D模型的制造就绪性提供了一种无需专业技能的交互式修复方案，混合交互界面是提升用户修复体验的关键设计方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI allow users to create 3D models from text or images. However, these models prioritize visual plausibility over geometric accuracy, often generating results with flaws that compromise their intended use post-fabrication. We present InstructMesh, an interactive post-generation refinement tool that enables selective repair of generative 3D models through region selection and targeted operations, such as opening or sealing voids, or adjusting local thickness. Users can invoke edit operations via natural language prompts or slider controls. By operating directly on the intermediate latent representation, InstructMesh allows users to apply robust geometric corrections without requiring expert modeling skills. To inform our design, we first analyze common fabrication-related failure modes in outputs from state-of-the-art generative tools. We then conduct two user studies, demonstrating that novices can identify and perform fabrication-relevant repairs on generative outputs using InstructMesh, and revealing user preference for hybrid interfaces that combine slider controls with natural language input.

</details>


### [227] [Logos: An Agent Harness on a Cross-Process Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28553)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Hao Cheng,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于时空可组合性演算，提出Logos——一个类似ROS的跨进程agent框架。它通过将插件作为独立进程、仅共享追加式日志的方式，解耦agent与单一进程绑定，实现了故障隔离（一次故障只在单个节点终止，而非中断所有共驻会话），实验证明其在工具调用周期的四个边界处被kill后，80个会话可无重复效果地恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代agent系统在运行时动态组装能力，已有的时空可组合性演算提供了完整的形式化处理，但其插件形式由单一进程承载、共享单一上下文，导致所有组件处于同一物理故障域——一次故障会挂起所有组件，进程死亡会中断其承载的所有会话。本文旨在证明该建模和演算并不将agent绑定到单一进程，从而打破这一故障域限制。

Method: 本文通过四个引理（其前提是演算的假设和语言模型推断的无状态性）论证：单一进程的建模并非本质约束，语言模型的无状态性使跨步状态全部保持在模型之外，健全性不变量仅基于状态空间定义。基于这些引理，构建了Logos——一个ROS风格的跨进程agent框架，其中插件是独立进程，唯一共享状态是追加式日志（append-only transcript）。

Result: 在工具调用周期的四个边界位置放置kill后，80个会话均可无重复效果地恢复；与单进程参考配置进行同故障对比显示：单进程配置下一次故障会中断所有共驻会话，而peer-process构架下一次故障只在一个节点终止。

Conclusion: 跨进程的agent架构能够在不违反演算健全性的前提下实现故障隔离，将故障影响从"全系统中断"降低到"单节点终止"，显著提升了agent系统的容错性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern agent systems assemble capabilities at runtime, and this dynamic composition has recently received a complete formal treat ment in the spatiotemporal-composability calculus, in which a capability is a component carrying a tracked inverse, and agents are assembled as plugins. This plugin form is carried by a single process sharing one context, a carrier that places all components in one physical failure domain, a fault suspends every component at once, and process death interrupts every session the process hosts. This paper shows that neither the modeling nor the calculus binds an agent to one process, the statelessness of the language model keeps all cross-step state outside the model, and the soundness invariant is defined on the state space alone. These observations condense into four lemmas whose premises are the hypotheses of the calculus and the statelessness of language-model inference. On these lemmas this paper constructs Logos, a ROS-like cross process agent harness in which a plugin is a process and the only shared state is an append-only transcript. Eighty sessions resume with no repeated effect after kills placed at the four boundaries of the tool-call cycle, and a same-fault comparison with a single process reference configuration shows one fault interrupting every co-resident session while under the peer-process construction one fault ends at one node.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [A Mixed-Behavior Vote Model for Multimedia Subjective Quality Votes, Means, and Variances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27724)
*Jaden Pieper,Stephen D. Voran*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了单峰方差区域（UVR）来更准确地描述多媒体主观测试中评分方差与均值的合法区域，指出了传统抛物线模型常违反该区域限制的问题，并提出符合该区域的替代模型。此外，作者提出了一种参数化的混合投票随机过程，可在任意MOS下产生UVR内的真实方差范围，并通过16个数据集（语音、图像、视频）验证了该模型的有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观测试中评分方差与均值（MOS）的关系已被广泛研究，但现有数学上可接受的方差区域过于宽松，无法准确反映真实的主观测评行为；同时常用的抛物线方差模型在方差-均值平面上往往违反合法性约束，为此需要更贴合实际且满足约束的方差区域定义和模型。

Method: 1) 提出缩减的可接受方差区域——单峰方差区域（UVR），更好地刻画多媒体主观评分的真实行为；2) 分析抛物线模型在方差vs MOS平面中违规的问题，并提出尊重UVR约束的替代模型；3) 提出一个参数化的随机投票过程，通过混合不同投票行为，在任何目标MOS下生成UVR内真实范围的投票方差。

Result: 在涵盖语音、图像和视频主观质量实验的16个数据集上进行了验证，结果表明所提出的UVR和投票模型能够较好地拟合真实投票行为，并在给定实验中提供更多可解释的洞察。

Conclusion: 该投票模型通过建模主观实验中的投票方差，为理解实验中的投票行为提供了额外的可解释性洞见；UVR和替代模型为方差建模提供了更符合实际且数学合法的框架。

Abstract: The relationship between subjective test vote variance and vote mean (or MOS) is well-studied, and the mathematically admissible vote variance region has been previously defined. We propose a reduced admissible variance region called the Unimodal Variance Region (UVR) that better describes real subjective rating behavior of multimedia. Further, subjective vote variance is often modeled as parabolic. We explain that, in practice, the parabolic model often violates the admissible region in the variance vs. MOS plane and we propose alternatives that respect the admissible region. We also present a parametrized random process to model votes that mixes voting processes and produces a realistic range of vote variances within the UVR at any desired MOS. This process was inspired by and comports with voting behavior that is observed in many subjective tests. By modeling vote variance from a subjective experiment, this vote model offers additional interpretable insights into voting behavior observed in a given experiment. We present example results from 16 datasets spanning speech, image, and video subjective quality experiments.

</details>


### [229] [Agentic Artifact Creation: Systems, Evaluation, Principles, and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28122)
*Tianfu Wang,Zhezheng Hao,Xilin Xia,Lixin Liu,Mengkang Hu,Hongzhang Liu,Xi Chen,Ziyan Liu,Xiankun Lin,Weijia Zhang,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 这篇综述调查了"代理式工件创作"（agentic artifact creation）领域，将其定义为AI系统通过状态化构建过程实质性构造或修订交付物的过程。作者回顾了截至2026年8月20日的259篇文献（230个系统和29个基准），比较了六种工件类型，分析了应用场景和评估实践，并提出了保持承诺和责任明确、将反馈转化为针对性修复、变更后重新验证受影响状态等原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽然能快速产生草稿和组件，但其实际影响力取决于这些碎片能否成为完整、可靠的交付物。现有研究多关注单次生成，缺乏对"从部分到完整交付物"这一状态化、迭代式构建过程的系统梳理，因此亟需一个统一的框架来理解和比较不同工件类型下的构建挑战。

Method: 作者首先定义了代理式工件创作的概念（状态化构建，中间观察可重定向后续工作），并从功能上将其解析为三个组成部分：工件操作表示、构建策略、以及能通过反馈重定向后续动作的运行时验证。随后对259篇文献进行系统综述，从六个工件家族（如图像、文本、代码等）进行分类比较，并将应用场景和评估实践作为独立维度进行交叉分析。

Result: 研究发现：构建挑战不仅取决于模态本身，还与决策耦合的紧密程度以及失败在可修复阶段是否可见有关；分解能降低局部复杂度但会增加协调和重组成本；学习型评判者与生成器共享偏好或盲区时，无法提供独立证据。基于这些发现，作者提出了保持承诺与责任明确、将反馈转为针对性修复、变更后重新验证受影响状态等设计原则。

Conclusion: 代理式工件创作需要超越单次生成的范式，关注构建过程中的状态管理、反馈循环和问责机制。作者呼吁在工件、创作者意图和构建系统共同演化的情况下，建立持续、连贯且可控的监督方式，并提供了公开的论文清单资源。

Abstract: Generative models can turn natural-language prompts into images, text, code, and other content, lowering the cost of producing drafts and components. Their practical impact increasingly depends on whether those pieces can become complete, dependable deliverables. This survey examines agentic artifact creation, which we define as stateful construction in which an AI system materially constructs or revises a deliverable and intermediate observations redirect later work. Functionally, the process links an operational representation of the artifact, a construction policy, and runtime verification whose feedback can redirect later actions. We reviewed 259 works available through August 20, 2026: 230 systems meeting this definition and 29 benchmarks of agentic artifact construction. We compare six artifact families, then analyze application settings and evaluation practice as separate dimensions. Across families, construction challenges reflect not only modality but also how tightly decisions are coupled and whether failures become visible while they remain repairable. Decomposition can reduce local complexity while increasing coordination and reassembly costs. Learned judges may add little independent evidence when they share the generator's preferences or blind spots. We formulate principles for keeping commitments and responsibility explicit, turning feedback into targeted repair, and revalidating affected state after change. We also identify opportunities for sustaining coherent, accountable control as artifacts, creator intent, and construction systems evolve. A curated paper list is available at https://github.com/GeminiLight/awesome-agentic-artifact-creation.

</details>


### [230] [MuSP-Bench: Advanced Multimodal Benchmarking of Music Understanding across Score and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28212)
*Milan Liessens Dujardin,Song-Ze Yu,Kevin Miao*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出 MuSP-Bench，一个包含 490 个问题的人类编写基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型对乐谱和演奏两种音乐模态的理解能力。评估显示前沿模型在理解乐谱方面存在困难，在推理演奏音频时挑战更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐家通过乐谱和演奏两种模态交流音乐：乐谱编码音乐意图，演奏将其以声音形式实现。作者希望探究多模态大语言模型能否有意义地处理这两种模态，填补该领域缺乏系统性评估基准的空白。

Method: 构建 MuSP-Bench 基准，包含 490 个人类编写的多选题，覆盖基于乐谱、基于演奏、解释性以及长时程推理等多种推理类型，涵盖古典钢琴和管弦乐作品。在多种输入条件下评估前沿多模态大语言模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，前沿多模态大语言模型在理解乐谱方面表现显著困难，而在对演奏音频进行推理时面临的挑战更为严峻。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在音乐双模态（乐谱与演奏）理解上仍存在明显不足，MuSP-Bench 可作为衡量该领域进展的基准，并公开供研究社区使用。

Abstract: Musicians commonly communicate music through scores and performances. Scores encode musical intent, while performances realize it in sound. To investigate whether models can meaningfully engage with both modalities, we introduce MuSP-Bench, a human-authored benchmark of 490 questions targeting understanding across Musical Scores and Performances. The benchmark distinguishes itself by spanning score-based, performance-based, interpretive, and long-horizon reasoning across classical piano and orchestral works. We evaluate frontier multimodal large language models under multiple input conditions. Our results show that these models struggle substantially to understand scores, while facing even greater challenges when reasoning about performance audio. The benchmark is available at https://musp.vaclis.net/.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [LitCurate: A Configuration-Driven AI-Assisted Framework for Scientific Database Construction with an Application to Lower-Mantle Equation-of-State Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27629)
*Abin Shakya,Wilson Samuels,Dominica Wilson,Gioia A. Marchi,Israa Draz,Chenxing Luo,Renata M. Wentzcovitch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LitCurate，一个基于大语言模型的开源框架，用于从科学文献中构建结构化数据库。该系统整合了文献发现、相关性筛选、全文处理和结构化信息提取，并保留中间结果和来源追踪，使人工审核和修订成为可能。作者将其应用于构建下地幔矿物相状态方程数据库，从205篇论文中提取了1,334条记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献中积累了大量实验和计算结果，但这些信息大多被锁定在出版物中，难以用于大规模分析和科学软件。从文献中构建结构化数据库的主要挑战在于：需要在大量论文中发现相关研究，并且提取的数量需要足够的科学背景才能保持可用性。

Method: LitCurate采用阶段式、可审计的策展工作流，整合了：(1)文献发现、(2)相关性筛选、(3)全文处理、(4)结构化信息提取四个阶段。系统保留中间结果和来源信息，允许研究者检查并修订各个阶段，而非将自动化策展视为黑箱过程。使用大语言模型驱动各阶段的处理。

Result: 应用LitCurate构建了包含下地幔及下地幔相关高压矿物相的状态方程数据库，包含来自205篇论文的1,334条记录。数据集将状态方程参数与矿物相、成分、方程形式、方法和参数约束相关联，并在可判定来源时标记值为来源报告或引用报告。数据通过可搜索的Web应用提供。

Conclusion: LitCurate通过将科学文献连接到可追踪的、机器可读的数据，为将积累的文献转化为科学分析和计算建模的资源提供了可复用的方法，解决了自动文献策展中可审计性和可修订性的关键问题。

Abstract: The growing scientific literature contains decades of experimental and computational results that could support data-driven and physics-based modeling, yet much of this infor- mation remains locked in publications and is not readily usable for large-scale analysis or sci- entific software. Building structured databases from the literature is particularly challenging whenrelevantstudiesmustfirstbediscoveredamonglargecollectionsofpapersandreported quantities must be extracted with enough scientific context to remain usable. We present LitCurate, an open-source framework for building scientific databases from the literature using large language models within an auditable, stage-wise curation workflow. LitCurate integratesliteraturediscovery, relevancescreening, full-textprocessing, andstructuredinfor- mation extraction while retaining intermediate results and provenance, allowing researchers to inspect and revise individual stages rather than treating automated curation as a black- box process. We apply LitCurate to construct an equation-of-state database of lower-mantle and lower-mantle-relevant high-pressure mineral phases from experimental and theoretical studies, comprising 1,334 entries from 205 papers. The resulting dataset links reported equation-of-state parameters to mineral phases, compositions, equation formulations, meth- ods, and parameter constraints, and labels values as source-reported or citation-reported when provenance can be determined. The records are available through a searchable web application. By connecting scientific literature to traceable, machine-readable data, LitCu- rate provides a reusable approach for transforming accumulated literature into resources for scientific analysis and computational modeling.

</details>


### [232] [Beyond the Vacuum: Combinatorial Strategy Selection for Competitor-Aware Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27631)
*Vaibhav Sourirajan,Yao Zhang,Himanshu Kumar,Sahil Wadhwa,Mann Patel,Amirfarrokh Iranitalab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将生成式引擎优化（GEO）形式化为竞争者感知的策略选择问题，并设计了两阶段流水线：首先使用BOCS高效搜索重写策略组合空间，然后利用BOCS黑盒观测生成偏好对和推理轨迹来微调语言模型，使其能够分析文档语料库并提出最优重写策略组合。该方法在多个曝光指标上达到SOTA性能，并展现出良好的跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GEO方法孤立地选择重写策略，忽略了关键的外部性：随着内容优化采用的增长，重写内容的最优策略会发生变化（即竞争者增加导致最优策略动态变化）。现有方法无法应对这种竞争环境下的策略选择问题。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(1) 使用贝叶斯优化组合结构（BOCS）高效搜索重写策略空间；(2) 从BOCS黑盒观测中生成偏好对和基于grounding的推理轨迹，微调语言模型以分析文档语料库并提出最优重写策略组合。

Result: 在geo-bench和合成的竞争数据集geo-bench_comp上，该方法在多个曝光指标上超越了现有的基于智能体和单一启发式的方法，达到SOTA性能；同时该方法能迁移到多个分布外数据集，在不同领域、查询和文档类型上均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 将竞争意识引入GEO是必要的，通过BOCS搜索与LM微调相结合的流水线能够有效解决竞争者感知的策略选择问题，并具备强大的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) has emerged as a novel paradigm for transforming content to increase visibility in Large Language Model (LLM) responses. Traditional GEO methods, however, select rewriting strategies in isolation, ignoring a critical externality: as adoption of content optimization grows, optimal strategies for rewriting content change. We formalize GEO as a competitor-aware strategy selection problem and propose a two-phase pipeline to solve it: (1) We use Bayesian Optimization of Combinatorial Structures (BOCS) to efficiently search the space of rewriting strategies, (2) We generate preference pairs and grounded reasoning traces from the BOCS black-box observations to fine-tune a language model to analyze a document corpus and propose optimal rewriting strategy combinations. We achieve state-of-the-art performance across several impression metrics over existing agentic and single-heuristic methods on both geo-bench and our synthetically augmented competitive dataset geo-bench_comp. Our method also transfers to multiple out-of-distribution datasets, proving effective across domains, queries, and document types.

</details>


### [233] [A Versioned Unified Graph Index for Dynamic Timestamp-Aware Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27663)
*Jun Woo Chung,Weijie Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TiGER（时间集成图高效检索），一种用于动态向量数据集上任意时间范围内快速时间感知近似最近邻搜索的新方法。通过构建和维护统一图结构，利用集成版本化连通性，可直接在统一图上查询任意时间区间，无需后过滤、合并或为每个时间片段构建子图。实验表明相比基线方法，QPS提升最高达5倍且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间感知的近似最近邻搜索方法在面对动态向量数据集时，要么需要构建每个时间段的独立子图（导致存储开销大、查询需合并），要么依赖后过滤机制（遍历大量无效向量导致效率低下），无法高效支持任意时间范围的查询需求。因此需要一个统一索引结构来直接处理任意时间区间查询。

Method: 通过构建和维护一个统一的图结构来索引所有向量，核心思想是利用"集成版本化连通性"（integrated versioned connectivity）作为索引机制。该结构使得任意时间间隔的查询可以直接在统一图上执行，无需遍历无效向量，从而避免了后过滤或合并操作，也无需为每个可能的时间组合范围创建单独的图。

Result: 实验评估表明，与基于过滤或基于每时间片段子图的基线方法相比，TiGER在每秒查询数（QPS）上实现了最高5倍的提升，同时不损失搜索精度。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在实时推荐系统、日志分析以及任何需要对动态时间分段数据进行快速相似性搜索的场景中，实现高效的时态分析。TiGER为动态向量的时间感知近邻搜索提供了一种统一、高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We present TiGER (Time-Integrated Graph for Efficient Retrieval), a novel approach for performing fast time-aware approximate nearest neighbor searches on dynamic vector datasets with flexibility over any possible time range. Our proposed algorithm builds and maintains a unified graph for all vectors by leveraging an index structure based on integrated versioned connectivity, allowing arbitrary time intervals to be queried directly on the unified graph without having to traverse invalid vectors. This forgoes the need for post-search filtering or merging, or separate graphs for each possible composite range. Empirical evaluations show that our method attains up to a 5x improvement in queries per second (QPS) without compromising accuracy over baselines based on filtering or per-time-segment sub-graphs. We believe that this method will enable efficient temporal analysis across evolving datasets in real-time recommendation systems, log analysis, and any scenario requiring fast similarity search over dynamic, time-segmented data.

</details>


### [234] [NormasTCU --- A Brazilian Portuguese IR Dataset and an Evaluation of LLM-as-a-Judge for Relevance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27746)
*Leandro Carísio Fernandes,Marcus Vinícius Borela de Castro,Leandro dos Santos Ribeiro,Leonardo Augusto da Silva Pacheco,Edans Flávius de Oliveira Sandes*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NormasTCU，一个巴西葡萄牙语法律信息检索数据集，包含14,469篇法律文档、46个查询和3,048条人工判断。作者评估了LLM作为评判者在葡萄牙语专业领域进行相关性评估的可靠性，发现LLM存在正向评分偏差（MAE 0.46-0.66），与人工判断的一致性仅为公平至中等水平（Cohen's kappa 0.32-0.53），但在nDCG@10和MRR等排序感知指标下能产生高度相似的系统排名（Kendall's tau ≥ 0.90），而在P@10和R@10下可靠性较差。结论是LLM可作为葡萄牙语专业语料库可扩展相关性评估支持工具，但应避免用于依赖精确率或召回率的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄牙语信息检索领域缺乏公开数据集，且专业领域（如法律）的相关性评估成本高昂。虽然LLM越来越多地被用于相关性评估，但其在非英语专业领域的可靠性尚不明确，需要系统性的评估研究。

Method: 构建NormasTCU数据集（14,469篇法律文档、46个查询、3,048个人工判断覆盖812个查询-文档对），使用三种LLM模型配合两种提示技术进行相关性评分，将LLM生成的qrels与人工参考qrels对比，评估15个IR系统的排名一致性，使用MAE、Cohen's kappa、Kendall's tau等指标进行衡量。

Result: LLM一致存在正向评分偏差（MAE 0.46-0.66）；与人工判断的一致性仅为公平至中等（kappa 0.32-0.53）；在nDCG@10和MRR下LLM生成的系统排名与参考排名高度相似（Kendall's tau ≥ 0.90），但在P@10和R@10下可靠性较差；LLM排名有时甚至比单个人类标注者与参考排名的相关性更强。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效支持葡萄牙语专业语料库的可扩展相关性评估，但前提是使用nDCG或MRR等排序感知指标；当依赖精确率或召回率时应避免使用LLM作为评判者。

Abstract: Portuguese Information Retrieval (IR) lacks public datasets, and relevance assessment for specialized collections remains costly. While Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly support relevance assessment, their reliability in non-English specialized domains remains unclear. We introduce NormasTCU (https://huggingface.co/datasets/LeandroRibeiro/NormasTCU), a Brazilian Portuguese IR dataset with 14,469 legal documents, 46 queries, and 3,048 human judgments over 812 query-document pairs. Using NormasTCU, we evaluated LLM-as-a-judge for relevance assessment by prompting three models with two prompt techniques to grade these pairs. We then compared the rankings of 15 IR systems derived from LLM-generated and human reference qrels. LLMs consistently showed a positive scoring bias (mean absolute error: 0.46--0.66 on a 0-2 scale). Furthermore, pair-level agreement with human judgments achieved only fair to moderate levels, with Cohen's kappa ranging from 0.32 to 0.53. Despite this bias, LLM-generated judgments often yielded highly similar system rankings for nDCG@10 and MRR (observed Kendall's tau greater than or equal 0.90, although the bootstrap confidence intervals did not always remain above this threshold), but were less reliable for P@10 and R@10. Notably, LLM-based rankings were sometimes more strongly correlated with the reference ranking than individual human annotations were. As a practical implication, our results suggest that LLMs could effectively support scalable relevance assessment in specialized Portuguese corpora when evaluated using nDCG or MRR (rank-aware metrics), but they should be avoided when relying on precision or recall.

</details>


### [235] [LINE Conversation History Retrieval for Personal Memory RAG: Evaluating Search Representations and Hybrid Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27809)
*Akito Hattori*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本论文是对个人LINE对话历史进行检索增强生成（RAG）的初步探索性研究，将358,896条消息切分为22,329个时间连贯的块，并构建了三种检索表示（原始文本、生成摘要、摘要加原始文本的embedding_text）。在100个评估问题上比较了BM25、稠密向量检索和线性混合检索，最终选定的embedding_text_bm25与embedding_text_vector组合（beta=0.45）达到了Recall@5=0.697。研究指出这是单一用户、单一标注者的探索性研究，在用于配置搜索的同一问题集上评估，未评估最终答案生成或对未见问题的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人记忆的检索增强生成（RAG）是LLM应用的重要方向，但已有研究很少关注如何在个人长期对话历史中进行有效检索。本研究旨在探索作为个人记忆RAG的第一步——仅关注检索环节，考察不同检索表示和混合策略在真实个人LINE对话历史中的表现。

Method: 将358,896条LINE消息分割为22,329个时间连贯的块；构建三种检索表示：raw_text、生成的摘要（summary）、以及结合摘要、原始文本摘录和固定文本的embedding_text。采用BM25、稠密向量检索和线性混合检索三种方式，在100个由单标注者验证的评估问题上进行比较；进一步探索6种检索器配对和21个权重，共126种配置。

Result: 单检索器中embedding_text_bm25表现最佳（Recall@5=0.584）；选定的embedding_text_bm25与embedding_text_vector组合在beta=0.45时达到Recall@5=0.697，MRR@5=0.595，nDCG@5=0.575，超越单检索器0.113（95%CI：[0.048,0.184]）。但聚合类问题（17个）的点估计值低于其他问题类型，表明当证据分散在多个时间和会话中时，扁平块级检索效果不佳。

Conclusion: 混合检索（结合摘要增强的BM25和向量检索）在个人对话历史检索中优于单一检索器，但研究存在显著局限：这是单用户单标注者的探索性研究，评估集与配置搜索使用同一组问题（存在过拟合风险），未涵盖最终答案生成环节，也未验证对未见问题的泛化能力。同时，当问题涉及跨时间、跨会话的分散证据时，扁平块级检索能力不足。

Abstract: As an initial step toward personal memory retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for large language models (LLMs), this study presents a retrieval-only case study over one user's LINE conversation history. We segmented 358,896 messages into 22,329 temporally coherent chunks and constructed three search representations: raw_text, a generated summary, and embedding_text, which combines a summary with a raw-text excerpt and other fixed text. We compared BM25, dense vector retrieval, and linear hybrid retrieval on 100 evaluation questions verified by a single annotator. Among individual retrievers, embedding_text_bm25 achieved the highest point estimate, with Recall@5 of 0.584. We then explored six retriever pairings and 21 weights, for 126 configurations on the same evaluation set. The selected combination of embedding_text_bm25 and embedding_text_vector at beta = 0.45 achieved Recall@5 = 0.697, MRR@5 = 0.595, and nDCG@5 = 0.575. Its Recall@5 exceeded that of embedding_text_bm25 by 0.113, with a question-level paired percentile-bootstrap 95% confidence interval of [0.048, 0.184]. This interval is conditional on fixing the configuration selected on the same 100 questions and does not account for uncertainty from configuration selection or weight search. The difference from a summary-based hybrid at beta = 0.50 was 0.050, with a 95% confidence interval of [-0.013, 0.115], so no clear difference could be established. The 17 aggregate questions also yielded lower point estimates than the other question types, suggesting that flat chunk-level retrieval struggles when evidence is distributed across multiple times and conversations. This evaluation is an exploratory single-user, single-annotator study conducted on the same question set used for configuration search; it does not evaluate final answer generation or generalization to unseen questions.

</details>


### [236] [Personalized and Multi-View Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27826)
*Jaehyung Lim,Wonbin Kweon,Woojoo Kim,Junyoung Kim,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PMFRec提出了一种面向联邦推荐冷启动场景的方法，通过个性化表示生成器和全局多视图编码器，将协同知识与属性知识融合为单一物品表示，在保护双端隐私的同时提升冷物品推荐性能、公平性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐方法假设物品池固定，忽视了新物品持续到达的冷启动场景。在双端约束（服务器不能访问客户端交互，客户端不能访问服务器专有物品属性特征）下，现有联邦冷启动方法存在三大结构性缺陷：缺乏个性化、将异质语义强行映射到单一嵌入空间导致组合性失败、以及分离式协同与属性表示的对齐导致训练和通信效率低下。

Method: PMFRec包含两个核心组件：(1) 个性化表示生成器，从属性特征生成用户特定的物品表示；(2) 全局多视图编码器，采用物品自适应门控和正交性目标来捕获互补的语义视图并减少跨视图冗余。此外，PMFRec将协同与属性知识融合为单一交换物品表示，无需显式客户端正则化器，降低了通信开销。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，PMFRec在冷物品推荐上持续优于强基线，并进一步改善了用户级公平性、暖场景适应性和本地差分隐私（LDP）下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PMFRec通过端到端的单一表示融合框架，同时解决了联邦冷启动推荐中的个性化、组合性和效率问题，为联邦推荐在动态物品池场景中的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated recommendation (FedRec) enables personalized modeling without centralizing users' interaction histories, but most existing methods assume a fixed item pool and thus overlook the practical cold-item setting where new items continuously arrive. Under the dual-sided constraint, where the server cannot access clients' interactions while clients cannot access the server's proprietary item attribute features, prior federated cold-start recommendation approaches suffer from three structural limitations: a lack of personalization, compositionality failure caused by forcing heterogeneous semantics into a single embedding space, and training- and communication-inefficiency arising from explicit alignment between separate collaborative and attribute representations. To address these challenges, we propose Personalized and Multi-view Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation (PMFRec). PMFRec learns a personalized representation generator to produce user-specific item representations from attribute features, and introduces a global multi-view encoder with item-adaptive gating and an orthogonality objective to capture complementary semantic views while reducing cross-view redundancy. In addition, PMFRec fuses collaborative and attribute knowledge into a single exchanged item representation, eliminating the need for an explicit client-side regularizer and reducing communication overhead. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that PMFRec consistently outperforms strong baselines in cold-item recommendation and further improves user-level fairness, warm-scenario adaptability, and robustness under Local Differential Privacy (LDP).

</details>


### [237] [ITER: Interaction-Aware Retrieval for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27912)
*Haodong Chen,Shuai Wang,Yu Yin,Shengyao Zhuang,Guido Zuccon,Teerapong Leelanupab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出iter，一个agent交互感知的稠密检索器。它利用agent轨迹学习信号进行训练，将主问题、当前子查询和先前子查询共同编码查询表示，并使用轨迹相对学习信号训练。在六个agent骨干和三个模型家族上，iter平均比现有方法LRAT在InfoSeek-Eval提升7.5%、BrowseComp-Plus提升13.5%，且比依赖外部LLM判断的AgentIR具有更强的跨agent鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索器训练仅依赖当前步骤的子查询和对应搜索结果作为训练信号，忽略了agent在先前交互步骤中积累的大量有用信息，导致这些历史交互信息未被充分利用。

Method: iter将查询表示构建为当前子查询、主问题和先前子查询的组合编码，并利用agent交互过程中产生的轨迹相对学习信号进行训练（包括将先前访问的有用文档用作后续搜索中的冗余负样本）。

Result: 在六个agent骨干（三个模型家族）上，iter平均相较于现有agent轨迹训练稠密检索器LRAT，在InfoSeek-Eval上提升7.5%，在BrowseComp-Plus上提升13.5%；同时展现出比依赖外部LLM判断信号和预搜索推理的AgentIR更强的跨agent鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 主问题和先前子查询构成最稳健的查询表示，而先前访问的有用文档作为冗余负样本提供了最强的轨迹相对监督信号。

Abstract: Deep-research agents answer complex user questions through an iterative sequence of search steps, where the agent autonomously formulates sub-queries to retrieve the evidence needed at each stage. However, existing retriever training typically relies only on the sub-query and its corresponding search results at the current step as training signals, leaving the information accumulated from previous interactions largely underutilized.
  We introduce iter, an agent interaction-aware dense retriever trained using agent trajectory learning signals. iter represents each query by incorporating not only the current sub-query, but also the main question and preceding sub-queries, and is trained using trajectory-relative learning signals derived from the agent's interactions.
  Across six agent backbones from three model families, iter consistently outperforms the existing agent-trajectory-trained dense retriever, LRAT, achieving an average improvement of 7.5% on InfoSeek-Eval and 13.5% on BrowseComp-Plus. iter also demonstrates stronger cross-agent robustness than AgentIR, a deep-research retriever that relies on external LLM-judge signals and the agent's pre-search reasoning. Ablations further show that the main question and previous sub-queries provide the most robust query representation, while previously visited and useful documents, used as redundancy negatives in subsequent searches, provide the strongest trajectory-relative supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/ielab/ITER.

</details>


### [238] [Information-Guided Selective Modality-Interest Alignment for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27950)
*Wenze Ma,Chenyu Sun,Yanmin Zhu,Qiwen Gu,Xuhao Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AMUR框架，一种信息引导的选择性模态-兴趣对齐方法，用于多模态推荐。它通过细化模态图结构并选择性对齐跨模态共享的与兴趣相关的语义，同时保留模态特有的互补信息，在三个真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMRec方法直接整合所有模态信息，但用户兴趣往往只与部分模态信号相关，其他信号可能引入噪声。已有方法（如不变学习、注意力机制、图细化、对比学习）的对齐过程多为隐式或启发式，缺乏明确目标来筛选与用户兴趣更匹配的模态信号。

Method: AMUR从信息论视角出发，首先根据用户行为细化模态图结构，然后选择性对齐跨模态共享的兴趣相关语义，从而增强与用户兴趣相关的模态信息，同时减少弱对齐信号的影响，并保留模态特有的互补信息。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，AMUR优于多个竞争性基线方法。

Conclusion: AMUR通过信息引导的选择性模态-兴趣对齐机制，有效解决了多模态推荐中模态信号与用户偏好错配的问题，验证了信息论驱动的模态筛选在推荐任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation (MMRec) aims to enhance recommendation performance by leveraging rich item content from multiple modalities. However, directly incorporating all modality information does not necessarily lead to better preference modeling, since user interests are often more related to a subset of modality signals, while other signals may be weakly aligned with user preferences or even introduce noise. Although recent MMRec methods improve modality utilization through invariant learning, attention mechanisms, graph refinement, or contrastive learning, their alignment processes are often implicit or heuristic and lack a clear objective for selecting modality signals that better match user interests.
  In this paper, we propose AMUR, an information-guided selective modality-interest alignment framework for multimodal recommendation. Inspired by an information-theoretic view, AMUR aims to enhance modality information that is more related to user interests while reducing the influence of less aligned signals. Specifically, AMUR first refines modality graph structures towards user behavior, and then selectively aligns shared interest-related semantics across modalities. This enables AMUR to improve modality-interest alignment while preserving useful modality-specific complementary information. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AMUR over competitive baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/Wenze1/AMUR.

</details>


### [239] [HubMixer: Progressive Latent Hub Mixing for Parameter-Efficient Feature Interaction in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27991)
*Jie Zhou,Zixian Gong,Wenhao Li,Chang Liu,Enzhao Shen,Bo Liu,Xu Guo,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HubMixer，一种参数高效的潜在中枢混合架构，用于推荐系统中的特征交互。通过"归纳-交互-读出"范式，HubMixer引入少量可学习的潜在中枢来组织特征交互，克服了直接混合异构token空间的参数低效问题。在工业推荐任务上超越SOTA，并在快手在线A/B测试中实现简历提交转化率5.48%的显著提升，已全面部署生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
