Motivation: 推荐系统中的token本质上是异构的：用户画像、物品属性、行为序列、上下文特征、统计信号和业务侧特征位于不同的语义空间，并以稀疏、样本特定的模式交互。直接在所有异构token空间中进行混合会导致参数低效，因为模型必须隐式发现哪些特征组应当交互以及如何路由这些交互。

Method: HubMixer采用"诱导-交互-读出"三阶段范式：(1) Hub诱导：通过交叉注意力让潜在中枢查询输入token，将异构token摘要为紧凑的潜在中枢；(2) Hub交互：在更干净的潜在中枢空间中进行高阶交互；(3) Token条件读出：每个原始token选择性从交互后的中枢读取信息，注入全局交互语义同时保留token级字段身份。

Result: 离线实验在工业推荐任务上超越SOTA模型；在快手短视频招聘业务的在线A/B测试中，简历提交转化率显著提升5.48%，且HubMixer已全面部署至生产环境。

Conclusion: 通过在异构原始token与直接混合之间引入紧凑的潜在中枢作为中介，HubMixer实现了参数高效的特征交互组织，证明了"先归纳再交互"的范式在处理异构推荐特征上的有效性，并在真实工业场景中取得了显著的业务收益。

Abstract: Learning effective feature interactions is central to industrial recommendation and advertising ranking systems. Recent token-mixing architectures simplify self-attention with lightweight mixing operators, improving hardware efficiency and enabling large-scale deployment. However, recommendation tokens are fundamentally heterogeneous: user profiles, item attributes, behavioral sequences, context features, statistical signals, and business-side features live in different semantic spaces and interact in sparse, sample-specific patterns. Directly mixing all tokens in the raw heterogeneous token space may therefore be parameter-inefficient, as the model must implicitly discover which feature groups should interact and how such interactions should be routed. In the paper, we propose HubMixer, a parameter-efficient latent hub mixing architecture for feature interaction in recommendation. Instead of directly mixing raw feature tokens, HubMixer introduces a small set of learnable latent hubs to organize feature interactions through an `induction--interaction--readout` paradigm. First, hub induction summarizes heterogeneous tokens into compact latent hubs, where latent hubs query input tokens through cross-attention. Second, hub interaction performs high-order interaction in the cleaner latent hub space. Third, token-conditioned readout lets each original token selectively read from the interacted hubs, injecting global interaction semantics while preserving token-level field identity. Extensive offline experiments on industrial recommendation tasks show that HubMixer outperforms the SOTA models. Online A/B testing in the Kuaishou short-video recruitment business further shows a statistically significant 5.48% improvement in resume submission conversion rate, and HubMixer has been fully deployed in production.

</details>


### [240] [Every Article Deserves a Video: Contextual Video Matching for Digital Publishers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28359)
*Arnaud Corone,Brice Pierre de la Briere,Gladys Roch,Samuel Leonardo Gracio,Yassine Bouher,Parvati Chauchaix*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"上下文视频匹配"系统，利用大语言模型（LLMs）和文本嵌入技术，自动将相关视频与文本文章进行匹配，解决数字出版商在大规模内容目录中手动选择嵌入视频的低效问题。该系统已在Dailymotion生产环境中部署，被数百家出版商采用，显著提升了用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字出版商面临大规模内容目录管理挑战，在文本文章中嵌入相关视频对货币化和用户留存至关重要，但手动选择在规模化场景下不现实，尤其是面对自有视频库或全球Dailymotion目录时。需要一种自动化的解决方案。

Method: 利用大语言模型（LLMs）和文本嵌入技术，构建自动将视频与文本网页/文章进行匹配的"Contextual Video Matching"系统，提供可扩展的解决方案。

Result: 系统已在Dailymotion生产环境部署，自上线以来已被数百家出版商采用，显著提高了用户参与度并丰富了用户体验。

Conclusion: 基于LLM和文本嵌入的自动视频匹配系统能够有效解决大规模出版场景下的视频-文章匹配问题，在实际生产环境中验证了其可行性和商业价值。

Abstract: As digital publishers face the challenge of managing massive content catalogs, the ability to effectively embed relevant video within text-based articles has become critical for both monetization and user retention. However, manual selection is impractical for large scale publishers, especially when navigating their own extensive video libraries or the entire global Dailymotion catalog. In this paper, we present the "Contextual Video Matching" system, a solution that automatically matches relevant videos with text-heavy web pages and articles. By leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) and textual embeddings, we provide a scalable solution for publishers to efficiently combine video content with their articles. We discuss in detail the motivations, architecture, evaluations, and deployment of this system within Dailymotion's production environment. Since its launch, the system has been adopted by hundreds of publishers, significantly increasing user engagement and enriching user experiences with highly relevant video content.

</details>


### [241] [SG-UMP: Sequence-Guided Universal Multimodal Prioritization Calculation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28503)
*Xinyi Zhang,Yutong Li,Peijie Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-UMP框架，一个即插即用的插件，通过模块组合器和模块路由器动态调整多模态信息处理流程，以解决现有MSR方法无法捕捉用户级偏好异质性和数据集级模态偏差的问题，在四个真实数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态序列推荐（MSR）方法在融合文本、图像和用户交互时，往往忽略了用户级别的偏好差异以及数据集级别的模态偏差，导致模型在不同用户和不同数据集上的适应性受限。

Method: SG-UMP包含两个核心组件：Module Combiner用于灵活的多模态信息处理，Module Router用于动态调整模块处理顺序，从而同时适配用户偏好和数据集特性，作为一个即插即用的插件集成到现有MSR模型中。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，SG-UMP在不同骨干网络和多模态设置下均能持续提升推荐性能。

Conclusion: SG-UMP通过动态自适应多模态信息处理，有效解决了用户级偏好异质性和数据集级模态偏差问题，为MSR提供了一种通用的性能增强方案。

Abstract: Multimodal sequential recommendation (MSR) improves recommendation by incorporating heterogeneous information such as text, images, and user interactions. However, existing MSR methods often fail to capture user-level preference heterogeneity and dataset-level modality bias, limiting their adaptability across users and datasets. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{S}equence-\textbf{G}uided \textbf{U}niversal \textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{P}rioritization Calculation Framework (\textbf{SG-UMP}), a plug-and-play plugin for enhancing multimodal information processing in MSR. SG-UMP includes a Module Combiner for flexible multimodal processing and a Module Router for dynamic module ordering, enabling adaptation to both user preferences and dataset characteristics. Experiments on four real-world datasets show that SG-UMP consistently improves recommendation performance across different backbones and multimodal settings. The code is available at https://github.com/esemsc-xz524/SG-UMP .

</details>


### [242] [QUEST: A Query and Extraction System for Topics in Asylum Law Application Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28555)
*Maria Vlachou,Anna Murphy Høgenhaug,Mohammad N. S. Jahromi,Galadrielle Humblot-Renaux,Thomas Gammeltoft-Hansen,Thomas B. Moeslund,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了QUEST系统，用于从丹麦庇护上诉案件中提取与可信度评估相关的因素。该系统将问题框架化为信息检索任务，结合合成查询生成、主题提取和相关性评估，并提出了基于可信度的领域特定评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 庇护申请的法律判决文件冗长、复杂且异构，当申请被拒绝时，判断原始申请的信息可信度是否是决定性因素是处理上诉的关键问题。传统方法难以自动从这些复杂文档中提取可信度相关的因素。

Method: QUEST系统采用信息检索框架，包含三个核心组件：合成查询生成（synthetic query generation）、主题提取（topic extraction）和相关性评估（relevance assessment）。此外，论文提出了区别于传统相关性度量的新型领域特定评估指标，专门针对可信度因素进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用基于可信度的相关性评估时，性能估计面临更大挑战，反映了该任务的难度。系统在不同类型的可信度指标上的检索效果存在差异。

Conclusion: 自动方法在返回庇护上诉中可信度相关答案方面取得了一定进展，但基于可信度的评估比传统相关性评估更具挑战性，说明可信度评估任务本身具有较高的复杂性。

Abstract: Legal decisions on asylum applications consist of long, complex, and heterogeneous documents, covering narrative applicant interviews, original decisions, and additional supporting materials. If an application is rejected, a critical question in processing an appeal is whether the credibility of the information in the original application was a factor that determined the original decision. In this paper, we present the QUEST system (Query and Extraction System for Topics) to extract and identify factors relating to credibility assessments in two datasets of Danish asylum application appeals. QUEST frames this problem as an information retrieval task, combining synthetic query generation, topic extraction, and relevance assessment to identify information related to credibility indicators in appeals board application materials. In addition to standard retrieval evaluation metrics, we propose a new type of domain-specific assessments distinct from the traditional relevance to evaluate the performance of the tested systems with respect to credibility factors. In this way, we obtain insights about how well automatic methods can return answers for different types of indicators appearing in asylum appeals. Our results indicate that there is an increased challenge when estimating performance using credibility-based relevance assessments, thus pointing to the difficulty of the task.

</details>


### [243] [PULSAR: Pooled Unified Late-Interaction Search and Retrieval for Enterprise Visual Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28572)
*Benjamin Constable,Anup Roy,Vishal Sharma,Rishabh Upadhyay,Robin Mills,Aidan Millar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PULSAR是一个部署于Mubadala投资公司的生产级视觉优先文档检索系统。它使用冻结的ColPali式骨干网络索引页面图像，并采用两级池化后期交互索引：先用紧凑页面摘要进行初步检索，再用细粒度池化表示进行精确MaxSim重排序。系统显著降低了向量搜索延迟（中位延迟156ms）和成本，并大幅提升了检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构投资者需要处理大量视觉密集的文档（如pitch deck、董事会材料、尽调材料），这些文档在交易临近截止时每小时都在变化。传统的OCR加图表文字化方法在高频刷新时成本高昂且会丢失图表细节，需要一种视觉优先的检索方案来解决这一问题。

Method: PULSAR采用视觉优先架构：1) 使用冻结的ColPali风格骨干网络对页面图像进行编码；2) 构建两级池化后期交互索引，其中紧凑页面摘要用于快速初始检索，随后在更细粒度的池化表示上进行精确MaxSim重排序；3) 采用事件驱动的数据摄取管道替代原来的OCR+文字化流程。

Result: 在ViDoRe V3基准上，池化设计相比非池化配置将中位向量搜索延迟降低15.1倍，NDCG@10和Recall@10损失均小于0.01绝对差值；生产环境的中位向量搜索延迟为156ms。并发负载下池化索引的QPS约为非池化索引的88倍。每页摄取成本比OCR+文字化基线约低20倍。自2026年3月起已服务7.8万份文档和约240万页面，覆盖超过3000笔交易，在生产的top K检索上，答案事实召回率是OCR+文字化基线的两倍以上。

Conclusion: PULSAR证明了在真实生产环境中，视觉优先的池化后期交互检索架构能够在保持甚至提升检索质量的同时，大幅降低延迟和成本，相比传统OCR+文字化方法具有显著优势，适合高频变动的机构投资文档检索场景。

Abstract: Institutional investors search visually dense pitch decks, board packs, and diligence materials that change hourly near deal closing. OCR followed by figure verbalisation is costly to refresh at this scale and can lose chart detail. We present PULSAR, a production vision-first retrieval system deployed at Mubadala Investment Company. PULSAR indexes page images with a frozen ColPali-style backbone and uses a pooled two-stage late-interaction index: compact page summaries support initial retrieval, followed by exact MaxSim rescoring over a finer pooled representation. On ViDoRe V3, this design reduces median vector-search latency by 15.1 times against an unpooled configuration with less than 0.01 absolute NDCG@10 and Recall@10 loss; production median vector-search latency is 156 ms. Under concurrent load, the pooled index sustains approximately 88 times higher QPS than an unpooled index. The event-driven ingestion path is estimated to be approximately 20 times cheaper per page than the OCR+verbalisation baseline it replaced. Since March 2026, PULSAR has served 78 thousand documents and approximately 2.4 million pages across more than 3,000 deals. At the production top K, it more than doubles answer-fact recall over the OCR+verbalisation baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Grounded Checklist Partial Credit for Agent Skill Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27487)
*Suliu Qin,Lu Yin,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Grounded Checklist Partial Credit (GCPC)，一种人类治理、LLM实例化的部分信用评估框架，用于评估语言模型智能体在长时程交互任务中的轨迹。人类定义可复用规则，LLM基于任务指令和官方验证器实例化任务级核查清单，评判者仅基于执行日志证据评分并在证据缺失时弃权。实验表明GCPC在区分PASS/FAIL、与人类评估对齐度以及揭露二进制结果掩盖的技能影响方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估依赖任务级二进制成功率（pass/fail），无法反映部分进度，尤其对程序性技能评估不利——技能可能改变执行方式但不改变最终结果。虽然核查清单能提供更细粒度评估，但手动编写成本高且自动生成不可靠，难以大规模可信评估。

Method: GCPC由三个阶段组成：(1) 人类定义可复用的评估规则；(2) LLM根据任务指令和官方验证器实例化任务特定的核查清单；(3) 评判者仅依据执行日志中的证据逐项评分，证据缺失时弃权；最后通过脚本步骤应用官方验证器结果到分数上，实现人类治理与LLM自动化的结合。

Result: 在4,455条轨迹的SkillsBench评估集上，GCPC比整体评判更好地区分官方PASS和FAIL结果（AUC 0.689 vs 0.619）。在96条轨迹（12个任务）的人类评估中，GCPC与人类进度评估更一致。在1,946个匹配的有/无技能配对中，879个配对二进制结果未变化的情况下，20.9%提升超过0.10，18.7%退步相同幅度，揭示了pass@1隐藏的效果。此外GCPC可迁移至Terminal-Bench和SWE-bench。

Conclusion: GCPC为智能体评估提供了一个可扩展、可信的部分信用评估框架，通过人类治理的规则定义和LLM驱动的清单实例化，在保持评估可靠性的同时揭示了传统二进制评估掩盖的细粒度技能影响，并展示了跨领域的适用性。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks in interactive environments, yet their evaluation commonly relies on task-level success rates by reducing an entire execution trajectory to whether the task passes an official verifier. This binary score hides partial progress and is particularly limited for procedural agent skill evaluations, since a skill can alter execution without changing the final outcome. While checklists provide finer-grained evaluation by scoring individual task requirements, costly manual authoring and unreliable automatic generation make trustworthy evaluation difficult to scale. To address these challenges, we introduce Grounded Checklist Partial Credit (GCPC), a human-governed and LLM-instantiated partial-credit evaluation of agent trajectories. Humans define reusable rules once, from which an LLM instantiates a task-specific checklist grounded in the task instruction and official verifier. To keep judgment tied to evidence, a judge scores each item from execution log evidence alone and abstains when evidence is missing. A separate scripted step then applies the official verifier outcome to the score. Across a 4,455-trajectory, deduplicated SkillsBench evaluation population, GCPC better discriminates official PASS and FAIL outcomes than holistic judging on the shared subset (AUC 0.689 vs. 0.619). Human evaluation on 96 trajectories from 12 tasks shows that GCPC aligns more closely with human assessments of progress. Applied to 1,946 matched with/without-skill pairs, GCPC exposes the effects hidden by pass@1: among 879 pairs whose binary outcome does not change, 20.9% improve by more than 0.10 while 18.7% regress by the same margin. The GCPC pipeline also transfers to Terminal-Bench and SWE-bench, demonstrating applicability beyond skill-conditioned evaluation.

</details>


### [245] [Image Augmentation as Test Generation for Deep Learning-Based Image Retrieval Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27502)
*Yehan De Silva,Anirudh Sridhar,Armin Lotfy,Nafiseh Kahani,Yvan Labiche,Ziyu Wang,Frank Ouyang,Clare Carty,Azalia Shamsaei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对深度学习图像检索系统的可靠性问题，提出了双重贡献：一是对50种图像增强/生成技术进行了文献综述并构建了十类分类体系；二是大规模实证研究，使用Amazon Titan和OpenCLIP嵌入模型，在三个数据集（CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1K和工业数据集）上从嵌入空间相似性、嵌入不确定性（四种估计器）、语义真实性和检索失败率四个维度评估这些技术。结果表明，天气模拟和SaSPA在产生最高嵌入不确定性和失败率的同时，保持了性能稳定性、视觉真实性和增强有效性的良好平衡；而GAN类技术真实度最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习图像检索系统的可靠性保障是软件工程领域的挑战，现有研究缺乏对图像增强/生成技术作为测试输入生成器的系统性评估。作者旨在通过文献综述和实证研究，为测试套件构建提供实用指南，同时通过变形测试降低人工标注成本。

Method: 研究分为两步：（1）系统文献综述，识别并分类50种增强/生成技术到十类分类体系；（2）大规模实证研究，使用Amazon Titan和OpenCLIP两种嵌入模型，在CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1K和March Networks工业数据集上，从四个维度（嵌入空间相似性、嵌入不确定性、语义真实性和检索失败率）评估这些技术作为测试生成器的效果。

Result: 在所有数据集和嵌入模型下，天气模拟和SaSPA产生最高的嵌入不确定性和检索失败率，同时在性能稳定性、视觉真实性和增强有效性之间保持良好平衡。相反，GAN类增强技术在真实性方面得分最低，存在合成伪影和感知不一致问题。结果具有配置特定性，可能因扰动强度设置不同而变化。

Conclusion: 研究为图像检索系统的测试套件构建提供了实用指南：选择最大化测试多样性同时保持真实图像特征的技术（如天气模拟和SaSPA），可构建全面有效的测试套件，并通过变形测试降低人工标注成本。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of deep learning-based image retrieval systems is a software engineering challenge. This paper presents a dual contribution: (1) a literature review of augmentation and generation techniques which resulted in the identification of 50 techniques which we organized into a ten-category taxonomy, and (2) a large-scale empirical study that evaluates these techniques as test generators for embedding-based image retrieval systems. Augmented images are embedded using Amazon Titan and OpenCLIP, and evaluated across four analytical dimensions: (1) embedding-space similarity, (2) embedding uncertainty measured via four estimators, (3) semantic realism scored by LLaVA, and (4) retrieval failure rate. Experiments are performed on three datasets: CIFAR-10, ImageNet-1K, and a dataset from an industrial partner (March Networks). Across all evaluated datasets and embedding models, and under the single severity level tested for each technique, weather simulation and SaSPA are the image augmentation/generation techniques that produce the highest embedding uncertainty and failure rates while maintaining a favorable balance between performance stability, visual realism, and augmentation effectiveness. The results we discuss are configuration-specific and may shift under milder or stronger perturbation settings. In contrast, GAN-based augmentation techniques are among the lowest in realism, indicating the presence of synthetic artifacts and perceptual inconsistencies that reduce their suitability to produce realistic test inputs. Overall, our findings provide practical guidelines for selecting augmentation techniques that maximize test diversity while preserving realistic image characteristics, thereby enabling the construction of comprehensive and effective test suites for image retrieval systems while reducing the cost of manual data labeling through the use of metamorphic testing.

</details>


### [246] [Predicting LLM Performance from Prompt Linguistic Features: An Empirical Study in Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27621)
*Quim Motger,Alessio Miaschi,Xavier Franch,Mohammad Amin Zadenoori,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究提示词的语言学属性能否在推理前预测LLM性能，从而降低提示词选择和优化的成本。通过对9,000个语言受控的提示词变体进行回归预测，发现语言特征显著预测提示词性能（R²在0.38-0.42之间），其中句法和词法句法特征贡献最大，跨模型预测因子包括复合依存分布、连词密度和词/句长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出对提示词措辞高度敏感，微小的措辞变化会显著影响输出质量。在软件工程中，提示词指导需求分析、代码生成和工件综合，但从业者缺乏在推理前评估提示词的原则性方法，导致提示词选择依赖昂贵的LLM调用和试错式优化。

Method: 从100个初始提示词生成9,000个语言受控的提示词变体，变化30个语言指标，用五个开源LLM在625个标注需求上进行评估。通过分层10折交叉验证训练回归预测器，使用基于置换的显著性检验，并利用特征重要性分析识别跨LLM和模型特定的预测因子。

Result: 语言特征在所有目标上显著预测提示词性能（R²在0.38-0.42，q<0.05）。句法和词法句法特征驱动大部分预测信号；跨LLM预测因子包括复合依存分布、连词密度和词/句长度，反映对领域词汇和复杂结构的敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究对提示词工程有实际启示：降低LLM性能的语言模式与增加人类理解困难的模式存在重叠，且词汇多样性作为质量维度无关紧要。更广泛地看，语言特征分析结合标准回归提供了在昂贵优化流程之前有效、可解释、低成本的先验评估手段。

Abstract: Background. LLM outputs are highly sensitive to prompt formulation: small wording changes can substantially affect output quality. This matters in software engineering, where prompts guide requirements analysis, code generation, and artefact synthesis. Poor formulations yield unreliable artefacts, yet practitioners lack principled ways to assess a prompt before inference, making selection depend on costly LLM calls and trial-and-error refinement. Aims. We investigate whether measurable linguistic properties of prompts can predict LLM performance before inference, enabling low-cost prompt selection and refinement, validated on binary requirements classification targeting F1, F2, precision, and recall. Method. We generate 9,000 linguistically controlled prompt variants from 100 initial prompts by varying 30 linguistic metrics, evaluated with five open-source LLMs on 625 annotated requirements. Regression predictors are trained via stratified 10-fold cross-validation with permutation-based significance testing; feature importance analysis identifies cross-LLM and model-specific predictors. Results. Linguistic features significantly predict prompt performance across all targets (R2 in [0.38,0.42], q<0.05). Syntactic and morphosyntactic features drive most predictive signal; cross-LLM predictors include compound dependency distribution, conjunction density, and word/sentence length, reflecting sensitivity to domain vocabulary and complex structures. Conclusions. Results suggest practical implications for prompt engineering, including overlap between linguistic patterns that reduce LLM performance and those that increase human comprehension difficulty, and the irrelevance of lexical variety as a quality dimension. More broadly, linguistic profiling combined with standard regression provides an effective, interpretable, low-cost prior before costly optimisation pipelines.

</details>


### [247] [Operationalizing Regulations into Code: A Model to Enhance Governance and Compliance in LLM Selection for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27703)
*Jonysberg Quintino,Hermano Moura,Filipe Calegário*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三层模型，通过设计科学研究方法（DSR）支持软件工程项目中LLM选型的治理与合规，结合欧盟AI法案、NIST AI RMF、GDPR等法规要求，采用多准则决策矩阵（含K.O.标准和加权评分）与PAG-LLM评估协议，并通过20个对抗性场景的试点评估验证了该模型在防止合规风险模型被选中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM集成到软件开发生命周期虽能提升生产力，但模型选型阶段面临安全、隐私和合规风险。现有法规（如EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、GDPR等）提出的义务难以转化为技术决策的可操作标准，需要一种治理导向的LLM选型支持模型。

Method: 采用设计科学研究（DSR）方法构建三层模型：（1）法规要求层；（2）组织治理能力层，通过带有K.O.标准和加权评分的多准则决策矩阵实例化；（3）生产力与可持续性结果层，由LLM治理评估协议（PAG-LLM）操作化。模型还包含将运营结果反馈至规范层的法规反馈回路。

Result: 基于CWE和OWASP Top 10的20个对抗性场景试点评估显示，商业云LLM与本地开源LLM存在不同的风险画像。法规淘汰逻辑（尤其是K.O.标准）能有效阻止选拔技术上具有竞争力但存在不可接受的合规风险的模型。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，治理导向的LLM选型在软件工程项目中是可行的，该模型能够将抽象的法规要求转化为可操作的技术决策标准，实现合规风险的有效前置拦截。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC) can improve developer productivity, but it also introduces security, privacy, and compliance risks during model selection. Regulations and frameworks such as the EU AI Act, the NIST AI Risk Management Framework (RMF), the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR), the Lei Geral de Proteção de Dados (LGPD), and ISO/IEC 42001 establish obligations that are often difficult to translate into operational criteria for technical decision-making. This paper proposes a model to support governance and compliance in LLM selection for software engineering projects. The model is developed through Design Science Research (DSR) and is structured in three layers: (i) regulatory requirements, (ii) organizational governance capabilities, instantiated by a multi-criteria decision matrix with knock-out and weighted scoring criteria, and (iii) productivity and sustainability outcomes, operationalized by the LLM governance assessment protocol (PAG-LLM). A regulatory feedback loop connects operational results back to the normative layer, enabling iterative refinement of the model. A pilot evaluation with 20 adversarial scenarios based on Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) and the OWASP Top 10 suggests distinct risk profiles between commercial cloud-based LLMs and local open-source LLMs. The results provide preliminary evidence that regulatory disqualification logic, particularly K.O. criteria, can prevent the selection of technically competitive models that nonetheless pose unacceptable compliance risks, demonstrating the feasibility of governance-oriented LLM selection in software engineering projects.

</details>


### [248] [Decoupling is a Necessity: Transformation-Agnostic Decompiled Code Recovery under Optimization and Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27889)
*Zhiping Zhou,Xiaohong Li,Ruitao Feng,Yao Zhang,Yuekang Li,Wenbu Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReSource是首个面向转换无关源码恢复的多阶段LLM框架，将二进制到源码的差异解耦为词法、句法和语义三个正交层级，通过经验先验检索、控制流平坦化预测和上下文词法推断三个解耦阶段，在超过8万对函数的基准上实现83%的Top-5源码检索准确率和0.66的平均相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的二进制代码同时遭受激进编译器优化和对抗性混淆，导致现有基于LLM的反编译工具经常出现结构崩溃和语义幻觉问题，无法可靠恢复高层程序逻辑。

Method: ReSource采用多阶段解耦策略：1) 从语义失真数据库检索经验先验以锚定LLM防止逻辑漂移；2) 集成轻量级预测器重构源码级结构骨架以解决控制流平坦化；3) 通过上下文词法推断阶段精炼标识符恢复可读性。

Result: 在包含8万+反编译源码函数对、覆盖三种优化级别和四种混淆技术的基准上，ReSource达到83%的Top-5源码检索准确率和0.66的平均相似度，优于DeGPT、LLM4Decompile和FidelityGPT等基线。

Conclusion: ReSource在现有强基线严重过拟合或性能退化的情况下仍保持稳健的语义可识别性，为下游安全分析提供了可扩展且可靠的基础。

Abstract: Reverse engineering is essential for software security analysis and vulnerability detection. Decompilation, the process of lifting binaries to high-level pseudocode, is central to this task. However, production binaries are hostile environments: aggressive compiler optimizations and adversarial obfuscation jointly mangle control structures, obscure variable intents, and disguise high-level program logic. Consequently, existing LLM-based decompilation tools frequently suffer from structural collapse and semantic hallucinations. We present ReSource, the first multi-phase LLM framework designed for transformation-agnostic source recovery. To tackle these intertwined distortions, ReSource conceptualizes the binary-to-source discrepancies into three orthogonal tiers, namely lexical, syntactic, and semantic, and decouples the recovery process accordingly. First, to ground the LLM and prevent logic drift, it retrieves empirical priors from a curated Semantic Distortion Database. Second, to resolve control-flow flattening, it integrates a lightweight predictor to reconstruct the source-level structural skeleton. Finally, a contextual lexical deduction stage refines identifiers to restore human readability. Evaluated on a massive benchmark of over 80,000 decompiled-source function pairs across three optimization levels and four obfuscation techniques, ReSource achieves an 83% Top-5 source retrieval accuracy and an average similarity score of 0.66. By maintaining robust semantic identifiability where state-of-the-art baselines (DeGPT, LLM4Decompile, and FidelityGPT) severely overfit or degrade, ReSource provides a scalable and reliable foundation for downstream security analysis.

</details>


### [249] [Antipatterns in AI-assisted Qualitative Data Analysis: A Catalog of Temptations and Pitfalls for Software Engineering Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27927)
*Rashina Hoda,Carolyn Seaman,Victoria Gomes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI辅助定性数据分析（QDA）中的反模式目录，旨在帮助软件工程（SE）研究者识别和避免在AI辅助定性研究中损害分析严谨性和效度的方法论风险。反模式分为三类：危险驱动因素、操作失误和分析失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助定性数据分析为SE研究带来巨大机遇，但不加批判的使用可能损害分析严谨性，并导致大量低质量研究的产生。目前SE研究者缺乏战略性的指导来识别和缓解AI辅助QDA中的方法论风险。

Method: 基于作者数十年的定性SE研究经验和专业知识，结合对AI辅助QDA新兴格局的理解，提出了一套AI辅助QDA的反模式目录，按照影响程度将反模式分为三类：Dangerous Drivers（危险驱动因素）、Operational Missteps（操作失误）和Analytical Failures（分析失败）。

Result: 提供了一个系统化的反模式目录，帮助SE研究者在尝试AI辅助QDA时识别和避免常见的诱惑与陷阱，同时为审稿人提供词汇和标准，以便指出有问题的或失败的研究实践。

Conclusion: 该反模式目录可作为负责任方法论演进的基石，推动定性研究中原则性和有意义的人机协作，促进SE研究领域向更严谨的AI辅助定性研究方向发展。

Abstract: AI-assisted qualitative data analysis (QDA) offers unprecedented opportunities to streamline software engineering (SE) research, yet uncritical use risks compromising analytical rigor and flooding the field with accelerated production of low-quality research. While tactical best practices will naturally evolve over time, SE researchers currently lack strategic guidance to identify and mitigate methodological risks when attempting AI-assisted QDA. Based on our decades of qualitative SE research expertise and experience combined with an understanding of the emerging landscape of AI-assisted QDA, this paper presents a catalog of antipatterns in AI-assisted QDA - a set of assumptions and practices that initially appear advantageous but ultimately undermine analytical rigor and validity. The antipatterns are grouped into three categories reflecting escalating impact: Dangerous Drivers, Operational Missteps, and Analytical Failures. As more SE researchers attempt AI-assisted QDA, these antipatterns will help them identify and avoid common temptations and pitfalls, while reviewers can be equipped with the vocabulary and criteria to call out problematic and failed practice. Ultimately, this catalog of antipatterns can serve as a stepping stone in our responsible methodological evolution toward principled and meaningful human-AI collaboration in qualitative research.

</details>


### [250] [CC4M: Code Clone Analysis and Visualization for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28111)
*Gen Kawamata,Yuki Ota,Norihiro Yoshida,Shiyu Yang,Erina Makihara,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CC4M，一个面向微服务的克隆分析与可视化工具。CC4M能够检测跨服务边界的代码克隆，并丰富其服务边界、共同修改、文件类别和度量信息，通过交互式散点图可视化，支持基于度量的过滤以优先处理高维护影响的克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构期望通过小规模、松散耦合的服务来支持软件演化，但已有研究表明服务边界之间存在代码克隆，部分克隆会在同一版本中被共同修改，导致修改需要跨服务边界传播，削弱服务独立性并增加维护成本。然而现有工具不支持微服务感知的克隆分析。

Method: CC4M工具首先检测跨服务边界的克隆对，然后为每个克隆对丰富服务边界信息、共同修改信息、文件类别信息和度量信息，最后在带有显式服务边界的交互式散点图中可视化这些克隆，支持基于度量的过滤以优先处理可能具有更高维护影响的克隆。

Result: 通过一个开源微服务应用案例，演示了CC4M如何帮助识别代码变更的潜在影响范围。

Conclusion: CC4M填补了微服务感知克隆分析工具的空白，为开发者提供了识别和维护跨服务边界代码克隆的有效手段，有助于降低微服务系统的维护成本。

Abstract: Microservice architecture supports software evolution by decomposing a system into small, loosely coupled services that can be deployed independently. Contrary to the expectation of high modularity, prior studies have reported that code clones exist across service boundaries, some of which are co-modified in the same version. Such clones may require changes to be propagated across service boundaries, thereby undermining service independence and increasing maintenance costs. However, existing tools do not support microservice-aware clone analysis. We present CC4M, a microservice-aware clone analysis and visualization tool. CC4M detects and enriches clone pairs with service-boundary, co-modification, file-category, and metric information. The enriched clones are visualized in an interactive scatter plot with explicit service boundaries, supporting metric-based filtering to prioritize clones with potentially higher maintenance impact. Using an open-source microservice application, we illustrate how CC4M helps identify the potential impact scope of code changes. A demo video and the tool are available at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0xOIQPFbkUg and https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21204195, respectively.

</details>


### [251] [RESTCov: A Tool for Structural Coverage Analysis of REST APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28114)
*Tolgahan Bardakci,Serge Demeyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RESTCov 是一个轻量级工具，通过 OpenAPI 规范和 HTTP 请求/响应日志计算结构化 REST API 覆盖率，无需源代码插桩，支持路径、操作、参数、媒体类型、状态码和状态类别的覆盖率报告，并生成可读的 HTML 报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REST API 在现代软件系统中广泛使用，但开发者和测试者往往缺乏对测试套件覆盖 API 规范哪些部分的可见性。传统覆盖率分析依赖源代码插桩，这对于分布式、外部维护且只能通过黑盒方式访问的 REST API 来说不切实际。

Method: RESTCov 从 OpenAPI 规范和观察到的 HTTP 请求/响应日志中计算结构化 REST API 覆盖率。它跨路径（paths）、操作（operations）、参数（parameters）、媒体类型（media types）、状态码（status codes）和状态类别（status classes）多个维度报告覆盖率，并生成机器可读结果和人类可读的 HTML 报告。

Result: RESTCov 能够帮助用户检查覆盖率缺口、诊断规范与日志之间的不匹配问题，并在无需访问实现的情况下评估 REST API 测试套件。工具已开源并提供录屏演示。

Conclusion: RESTCov 提供了一种无需源代码插桩的轻量级 REST API 覆盖率分析方法，填补了黑盒环境下 API 测试覆盖率评估的空白，对开发和测试实践具有实用价值。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used in modern software systems, but developers and testers often lack visibility into which parts of an API specification are exercised by a test suite. Traditional coverage analysis usually relies on source-code instrumentation, which is impractical for REST APIs that are distributed, externally maintained, and hence accessible only through black-box execution. This paper presents RESTCov, a lightweight tool that computes structural REST API coverage from an OpenAPI specification and observed HTTP request/response logs, reporting coverage across paths, operations, parameters, media types, status codes, and status classes. RESTCov produces both machine-readable results and a human-readable HTML report, helping users inspect coverage gaps, diagnose specification-log mismatches, and evaluate REST API test suites without requiring access to the implementation.
  Screencast: https://youtu.be/mNz2P43OyUc
  Repository: https://github.com/2tolgahan2/RESTCov

</details>


### [252] [Post-Edit Re-Verification in Simulator-Backed Engineering Agents: A Controlled Comparison of Verification-Cadence Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28147)
*Qingchuan Zhu,Shuyue Tong,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对照实验考察在仿真器交互中，保留（CG）与省略（CO）明确的"编辑后重新验证节奏"指令对首次后编辑重新验证行为的影响。实验在DWSIM仿真器上进行，共进行了120个评估槽位每条件，结果显示CG条件下重新验证率（94/120 vs 32/120）、违规率（26/120 vs 87/120）和最终成功率（95/120 vs 35/120）均显著优于CO条件，从而支持将验证节奏视为显式交互协议组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程智能体与外部仿真器交互时，在进行设计修改后需要重新获取工程证据，但当前不清楚显式的验证节奏指导是否会影响智能体的首次编辑后重新验证行为。作者希望回答"编辑后首次再验证是否因显式验证节奏指导的保留与否而改变"这一问题，为将验证节奏视为交互协议组件提供实证依据。

Method: 采用对照实验设计：CG条件保留"实质性修改后请求新仿真"的指令，CO条件移除该指令，两者均不设硬性门控。以DWSIM为仿真后端，以连续阀压调整为任务，使用五个阿里/Qwen模型在八个合成案例上评估，每个模型-案例-条件组合通过实时API调用执行三次，形成每条件120个评估槽位。测量重新验证发生次数、节奏违规次数和有界最终成功率。

Result: CG条件在120个槽位中观察到94次重新验证，而CO条件仅32次；节奏违规分别为26次与87次；有界最终成功分别为95次与35次。其中qwen3.5-35b-a3b模型在CG中仅1/24次重新验证、CO中0/24次，且两种条件下均未取得最终成功。总体而言，显式后编辑验证节奏指导与更多重新验证、更少节奏违规和更高有界最终成功率相关。

Conclusion: 在有界协议范围内，显式保留后编辑验证节奏指导能显著改善智能体的重新验证行为与最终任务成功率，支持将验证节奏作为显式交互协议组件的处理方式。

Abstract: Engineering agents that interact with external simulators may need to coordinate design modification with reacquisition of engineering evidence for the modified state. We ask whether first post-edit re-verification changes when explicit verification-cadence guidance is retained versus omitted while verification-relevant state/facts are held constant. Cadence-Guided (CG) retained an instruction to request a new simulation after a substantive modification, whereas Cadence-Omitted (CO) removed that instruction; neither condition used a hard gate. The study therefore measures instruction-conditioned post-edit verification-policy adherence rather than spontaneous recognition that prior evidence has become stale. Using DWSIM as the simulator backend and continuous valve-pressure adjustment, five Alibaba/Qwen models were evaluated on eight synthetic cases; each model-case-condition combination was executed three times via live API calls, yielding 120 evaluation slots per condition. Re-verification was observed in 94/120 CG slots versus 32/120 CO slots; cadence violations occurred in 26/120 versus 87/120; and bounded final success was reached in 95/120 versus 35/120. qwen3.5-35b-a3b showed minimal re-verification (1/24 in CG and 0/24 in CO) and no final success in either condition. Within this bounded protocol, explicit post-edit verification-cadence guidance was associated with more re-verification, fewer cadence violations, and more frequent bounded final success, supporting the treatment of verification cadence as an explicit interaction-protocol component.

</details>


### [253] [From Architecture to Binary: Ensuring Cross-Domain Consistency in Model-Based Airborne Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28156)
*Nils Schlautmann,Viktor Sinitsyn,Benjamin Engelhard,Florian Holzapfel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向有人/无人飞行器的机载软件开发方法，通过以仓库为中心的架构减少系统域、基于模型的功能域和嵌入式软件域之间的不一致性。该方法基于关系接口数据库工具链，为每个域分配主仓库，并通过跨仓库引用和专用CI流水线自动生成、更新和验证交换工件，在耗时步骤前暴露不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ARP-4754B和DO-178C等标准影响下，系统域、模型功能域和嵌入式软件域之间的不一致性问题普遍存在。这些问题主要源于跨域边界的约束执行不足，而非流程定义缺失。对于资源受限的小型团队，需要一种无需重型流程开销的解决方案来减少跨域不一致性。

Method: 基于此前提出的以关系接口数据库为核心的工具链，识别常见故障模式，提出仓库中心化实现方案。每个域分配一个主仓库，通过跨仓库引用建立关联，并配置专用CI流水线用于生成、更新和验证交换工件。实现自动化接口更新、差异变更通知和一致性检查，在代码生成和编译之前发现不一致问题。

Result: 在正在进行的实验项目中进行了初步实现，并获得了早期使用的定性反馈。初步结果显示该方法能够以最小的人工工作量传播变更并提前暴露跨域不一致问题。

Conclusion: 面向小规模、资源受限团队，该仓库中心的跨域一致性问题解决方案避免了重型流程开销，通过自动化传播和预编译阶段检查有效减少系统、功能和软件域之间的不一致性。

Abstract: This paper presents an airborne software development approach for manned and unmanned aerial vehicles aimed at reducing inconsistencies across system, model-based functional, and embedded software domains. In environments influenced by standards such as ARP-4754B and DO-178C, these inconsistencies typically stem from insufficient enforcement across domain boundaries rather than missing process definitions. Building on a previously proposed toolchain centered on a relational interface database, we identify recurring failure modes and propose a repository-centered implementation to address them, tailored to small, resource-constrained teams operating without heavyweight process overhead. Each domain is assigned a primary repository with cross-repository references and dedicated CI pipelines that generate, update, and validate the exchanged artifacts. Automated interface updates, differential change notifications, and consistency checks propagate changes with minimal manual effort and surface inconsistencies before the time-consuming code-generation and compilation steps. An initial implementation in an ongoing experimental project is described, with qualitative feedback from its early use.

</details>


### [254] [Adaptive Strategy Generation for Boundary Value Exploration Beyond Numeric Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28230)
*Sabinakhon Akbarova,Felix Dobslaw,Robert Feldt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ABEX，一个基于LLM智能体的边界值探索（BVE）框架。它用自适应策略生成替代传统的人工设计变异算子，通过LLM智能体提出、选择和执行边界探索策略，并由执行反馈和质量多样性（QD）档案引导。在20个函数（数值、字符串、数组和混合输入）的黑盒测试中，ABEX在数值函数上平均QD分数比最先进方法高11.7倍，在非数值函数上首次实现自动化边界发现，变异分数达86.2%（对比基线61.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件行为常在输入区域边界处发生突变，这些边界区域容易出错。现有自动化边界值探索（BVE）技术依赖针对每个输入类型甚至每个被测函数手工设计的变异算子，这使其仅限于数值输入，无法扩展到字符串、数组等非数值类型。作者希望通过自适应策略生成来替代算子工程，从而突破这一限制。

Method: ABEX框架包含专门化的LLM智能体，这些智能体以自然语言表达边界探索策略，能够编码类型级和函数特定的知识。框架流程包括：智能体提出策略、选择和执行策略，并由执行反馈和质量多样性（QD）档案进行引导。有效的策略可以被存储和复用。整个方法在黑盒设置下运行，不需要源代码访问。

Result: 在20个函数上评估：数值函数上，ABEX在11个函数中的10个上优于最先进的QD方法，平均QD分数高出11.7倍；非数值函数上对所有测试对象均发现域对齐的边界行为；变异测试显示ABEX平均变异分数为86.2%对比QD基线的61.9%，杀死的顽固变异体数量是基线的9倍；消融研究确认自适应策略生成是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: ABEX通过以LLM智能体的自适应策略生成替代人工算子工程，成功将自动化黑盒边界值探索从数值输入扩展到非数值输入，并显著提升了边界发现的质量和缺陷检测能力，证明智能体驱动的策略生成是替代传统算子工程的有效范式。

Abstract: Software behavior often changes abruptly at boundaries between input regions, and these transitions are known to be fault-prone. Boundary Value Exploration (BVE) automates boundary discovery by searching for pairs of similar inputs that nevertheless trigger different program behaviors. Existing automated BVE techniques rely on mutation operators hand-engineered for each input type, or even for each function under test, which has confined their use to numeric inputs. We present ABEX, an agentic LLM-based framework that replaces operator engineering with adaptive strategy generation: specialized LLM agents propose, select, and execute boundary-exploration strategies, guided by execution feedback and a quality-diversity (QD) archive. Because strategies are expressed in natural language, they can encode both type-level and function-specific knowledge, and effective strategies can even be stored and reused. We evaluate ABEX in a black-box setting on 20 functions with numeric, string, array, and mixed inputs. On numeric functions, ABEX outperforms a state-of-the-art QD method on 10 of 11 functions, with average QD-scores 11.7x higher. On non-numeric functions, addressed here for the first time in automated black-box BVE, ABEX discovers domain-aligned boundary behaviors for all subjects. Mutation testing shows the discovered boundaries are fault-revealing: with equally sized test suites, ABEX reaches an average mutation score of 86.2% versus 61.9% for the QD baseline, and kills nine times as many hard-to-detect stubborn mutants. An ablation study identifies adaptive strategy generation as the primary driver of these gains.

</details>


### [255] [Where Does Balance Break? Boundary Discovery for Game Balance Testing under a Finite Simulation Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28364)
*Hiroki Mukai,Yusaku Kato,Norihiro Yoshida,Erina Makihara,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BBExplorer，一种在有限仿真预算下将游戏平衡回归测试形式化为边界发现问题的方法，通过多方向候选生成、预算感知两阶段筛选和自适应步长收缩来高效定位平衡与非平衡区域边界，实验证明其在低维和高维场景下均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统存在非确定性执行和巨大的行为空间，使得穷举探索不切实际、单次运行判断不可靠。竞技多人游戏是这类系统的典型代表，其平衡性至关重要且对参数变化敏感，但检测平衡破坏需要大量重复仿真。现有方法难以高效地在高维参数空间中识别平衡与不平衡区域的边界。

Method: 将游戏平衡回归测试形式化为有限仿真预算下的边界发现问题。提出BBExplorer，包含三个核心组件：(1) 多方向候选生成，在多个方向探索潜在边界附近输入；(2) 预算感知的两阶段筛选，高效分配有限仿真资源以区分候选点；(3) 自适应步长收缩，逐步细化边界精度。

Result: 在两个不同复杂度级别的游戏上进行实验，结果表明该方法在低维设置下表现强劲，在高维设置下仍保持有效。此外，在未见过的随机种子和阈值设置下展现出稳定的边界行为。

Conclusion: BBExplorer对实际的平衡回归测试有效，并更广泛地适用于非确定性、预算受限系统中的面向边界的测试。该方法在有限仿真资源约束下能够可靠地识别平衡边界，为游戏平衡性测试提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Software testing often relies on assumptions such as reproducible executions and stable correctness criteria. However, many modern software systems exhibit non-deterministic executions and large behavior spaces, making exhaustive exploration impractical and single-run judgments unreliable. These characteristics make it difficult to identify where acceptable behavior ends and problematic behavior begins. Competitive multiplayer games represent a challenging instance of such systems, where balance must be maintained so that no single strategy dominates. Even small parameter changes can trigger abrupt balance disruption, yet detecting such failures requires repeated simulations under non-deterministic outcomes and high-dimensional parameter spaces. In this paper, we formulate game balance regression testing as a boundary-discovery problem under a finite simulation budget. The objective is to efficiently identify inputs near the boundary that separates balanced and unbalanced regions. To address this problem, we propose BBExplorer, which combines multi-directional candidate generation, budget-aware two-stage screening, and adaptive step-size shrinkage for boundary refinement. Experimental results on two games with different levels of complexity show that the approach is strong in low-dimensional settings and remains effective in higher-dimensional ones. It also exhibits stable boundary behavior across unseen random seeds and threshold settings. These results indicate that BBExplorer is effective for practical balance regression testing and, more broadly, for boundary-oriented testing in non-deterministic, budget-constrained systems.

</details>


### [256] [Sustainability of Open-Source Machine Learning Robustness Assessment Tools: A Repository Mining Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28396)
*Joshua Owotogbe,Indika Kumara,Willem-Jan van den Heuvel,Damian Tamburri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源鲁棒性评估工具生态系统进行了实证研究，从28个GitHub仓库中分析社区参与度、维护活动和项目寿命，发现参与度和维护水平分布不均，仅有5个仓库活跃、22个不活跃、1个已归档，强调这些工具应被视为持续演进的软件系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管鲁棒性评估工具（如ART、Foolbox、Robustness Gym）被广泛用于真实世界ML系统的部署，但关于这些工具的维护状况、社区参与度和长期可持续性的研究几乎空白。从业者需要依赖这些信息来选择评估依赖、复现鲁棒性评估以及提供AI保证证据。

Method: 从先前工作中精选种子集出发，在GitHub上系统搜索并识别出28个鲁棒性工具仓库，采用既定的软件工程指标分析仓库制品，以刻画社区参与度、维护活动和项目寿命。

Result: 社区参与度和维护活动分布不均，持续活跃集中在少数仓库。截至2026年1月21日，5个仓库被分类为活跃状态，22个为不活跃，1个已归档。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性工具应被视为不断演进的软件系统而非静态资源，研究结果强调了在工具选择和维护策略上需要更强的意识和治理，以确保鲁棒性评估的可靠性和可持续性。

Abstract: Robustness evaluation is essential for deploying machine-learning (ML) systems in real-world settings, where models may face adversarial perturbations, distribution shifts, and other operational stressors. Many open-source tools, including Adversarial Robustness Toolbox, Foolbox, and Robustness Gym, support robustness testing and evaluation. However, little is known about how these tools are maintained, publicly engaged with, and sustained over time, even though practitioners may rely on them to select evaluation dependencies, reproduce robustness assessments, and provide evidence for AI assurance. We present an empirical study of the open-source robustness tooling ecosystem. Starting from a curated seed set derived from prior work, we systematically searched GitHub and identified 28 robustness-tool repositories. We analyzed repository artifacts to characterize observable community engagement, maintenance activity, and project longevity using established software-engineering metrics. Our results show that engagement and maintenance are unevenly distributed, with sustained activity concentrated in a small subset of repositories. At the data collection date of January 21, 2026, five repositories were classified as active, 22 as inactive, and one as archived. These findings highlight the need to treat robustness tools as evolving software systems.

</details>


### [257] [Recovering Software Architecture Intent from Historical Work Items using Generative AI: A Mixed-Methods Industry Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28403)
*Dominik Storck,Tobias Eisenreich,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的五步半自动流水线，从Azure DevOps历史敏捷工作项（用户故事等）中恢复C4架构图。在两个工业项目上通过专家访谈（定性）和稳定性分析（定量）评估，结果显示生成架构基线准确且对系统理解有用，能揭示架构漂移；实体稳定性高但关系稳定性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构往往仅部分体现在代码中，大量设计意图存于不断演化的项目制品中。敏捷项目的工作项、用户故事等跟踪文档保留了这些意图的宝贵痕迹，但很少支持直接的架构分析，因此需要一种方法从这些非结构化制品中恢复架构视图。

Method: 采用基于LLM的流水线，包含五个半自动步骤：提示链（prompt chain）、双向可追溯性（bidirectional traceability）和思维链推理（Chain-of-Thought），将非结构化的Azure DevOps工作项转换为C4架构图视觉制品。

Result: 在两个工业项目上采用混合方法评估：定性上，从业者认为生成的架构基线准确且对系统理解高度有用；制品严格受限于输入数据，能揭示与实现现实的差异和架构漂移。定量上，流水线对架构实体表现出高稳定性，但对实体间关系稳定性较低，相对方差在生成步骤间累积放大。

Conclusion: 该工作流证明了基于开发过程制品进行LLM辅助架构恢复的实践可行性，为敏捷项目中架构理解与漂移检测提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Software architecture is often only partially captured in code, while much of the design intent lives in evolving project artifacts. In agile projects, work items, user stories, and related tracking documents preserve valuable traces of that intent, but they rarely support direct architectural analysis. This work investigates the recovery of C4 architecture diagrams from historical agile work items using an LLM-based pipeline. The semi-automatic five-step workflow employs a prompt chain, bidirectional traceability, and Chain-of-Thought reasoning to transform unstructured Azure DevOps work items into visual artifacts. Evaluated on two industry projects, we use a mixed-methods design combining qualitative expert interviews with a quantitative stability analysis. Practitioners perceive the generated architectural baselines as accurate and highly useful for system comprehension. Strictly bound by their input data, the artifacts mirror the documented intent, thereby surfacing discrepancies and architectural drift when compared to the implemented reality. Quantitatively, the workflow exhibits high stability for architectural entities but lower stability for their relationships, with relative variance compounding across generation steps. The proposed workflow demonstrates the practical viability of LLM-assisted architectural recovery based on development process artifacts.

</details>


### [258] [On the Maintenance and Co-evolution of Agent Plugins: An Empirical Study of Claude Code Plugin Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28497)
*Ahmed Hereiz,Yingzhe Lyu,Hao Li,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对1,926个Claude Code插件市场仓库、8,351个插件和77,773次提交进行了实证分析，发现插件市场快速扩张（6个月内提交活动增长8.8倍），开发以功能驱动为主（39.6%功能提交，远超传统开源软件的17.2%），Claude参与合著34.9%的提交。在技能目录中，自然语言指令文件和实现脚本以高于随机概率的频率共同演化，78%的共变更具有功能耦合，代表一种传统软件工程中未观察到的新型维护依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理的插件市场正在快速发展，但与传统软件包不同，代理插件通过自然语言指令文件、脚本和配置文件的组合来交付功能。研究者希望探究这些插件是持续维护的构件（各组件间共同演化），还是开发者一次性编写后不再修改的一次性产物。

Method: 对1,926个托管Claude Code插件市场的仓库进行实证研究，分析8,351个插件、77,773次提交，覆盖2,018个市场。通过提交活动分析、提交类型分布统计、AI合著比例分析以及组件间共同演化分析，与传统开源软件进行对比。

Result: 插件市场快速扩张，2025年10月发布后6个月内插件相关的提交活动增长8.8倍；软件工程任务类插件占61.3%；功能提交率（39.6%）是传统开源软件（17.2%）的两倍以上；Claude合著34.9%的提交；docs、perf、style、refactor四种提交类型在插件仓库中含义与传统软件显著不同；技能目录中指令文件和实现脚本以高于偶然水平的比率共同演化，其中78%的共变更具有功能耦合。

Conclusion: 代理插件是一个活跃维护且不断演化的生态系统，其中自然语言指令与实现脚本之间的功能耦合共同演化构成了一类全新的维护依赖关系，这在传统软件工程中不存在，需要新的维护工具和实践来支持。

Abstract: AI coding agents, software tools that automate development tasks through reasoning and tool use, are increasingly extended through plugin marketplaces, yet the structure, maintenance, and co-evolution dynamics of these emerging repositories remain empirically unexplored. Unlike traditional software packages that deliver functionality through source code, agent plugins deliver functionality through a combination of natural-language instruction files, scripts, and configuration files, raising the question of whether these plugins are maintained artifacts that co-evolve across components, or one-off artifacts that developers write once and do not need to revisit. To study the maintenance and co-evolution of agent plugins, we conduct an empirical study of 1,926 repositories hosting Claude Code plugin marketplaces, analyzing 8,351 plugins and 77,773 commits across 2,018 marketplaces. We find that the marketplace is expanding rapidly, plugin-touching commit activity growing 8.8x over six months after the October 2025 launch, and plugins targeting Software Engineering tasks accounting for 61.3% of all plugins. Plugin development is predominantly feature-driven, with feature commits occurring at more than twice the rate of conventional open-source software (OSS) (39.6% vs. 17.2%). Claude co-authors 34.9% of all commits, and four commit types (docs, perf, style, and refactor) carry substantially different meanings in plugin repositories than in traditional software. Most component types evolve independently, but within skills directories, natural-language instruction files and implementation scripts co-evolve at above-chance rates, with 78% of co-changes being functionally coupled, representing a new class of maintenance dependency not observed in traditional software engineering.

</details>


### [259] [A System-of-Systems Case Study for the Verification of Composed Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28498)
*Mennatullah T. Khedr,Mengwei Xu,John S. Fitzgerald,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在网络物理系统（CPS）中工程化可靠数字孪生（DT）的方法论缺失问题，特别聚焦于系统之系统（SoS）场景下DT质量的正式化与验证。通过一个基于VDM-RT建模的温室SoS案例研究，作者将DT质量（如相关性、可验证性、可替代性和保真度）操作化为可验证的属性集合，并识别了在SoS层面组合推理时面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的DT工程方法缺乏关于如何正式化和验证质量属性（如相关性、可验证性、可替代性和保真度）的实用指导。在系统之系统（SoS）环境中，这种需求尤为迫切，因为可靠性依赖于多个DT的组合。现有研究未能系统性地解决这些问题。

Method: 作者采用案例研究方法，构建了一个基于温室SoS的数字孪生系统，使用VDM-RT（维也纳开发方法-实时）进行建模。该方法包括：（1）创建可执行的正式模型；（2）将DT质量属性操作化为可验证的属性集；（3）分析在SoS层面组合这些构件时遇到的障碍；（4）识别支持验证的正式建模与分析工具。

Result: 研究揭示了DT质量解释必须适应不同架构角色，本地质量保证构成组合推理的假设。案例研究表明，个体DT验证是可行的，但完整的SoS组合正式化和验证仍存在重大挑战。VDM-RT等正式建模工具可以有效支持个体DT属性的验证。

Conclusion: 本文认为，为SoS环境中的DT开发一个综合的验证与确认框架是必要的，个体DT构件的正式化是迈向组合推理的关键一步。完全的SoS组合正式化与验证被认定为未来研究方向。

Abstract: Current approaches to engineering dependable Digital Twins (DTs) of Cyber-Physical Systems lack practical guidance on how qualities such as relevance, verifiability, substitutability and fidelity may be formalised and verified. This need is amplified in Systems-of-Systems (SoS), where reliance is placed on the composition of DTs. The goal of this study is to identify foundational challenges that a framework for DT validation and verification should address in an SoS setting, and to investigate the formalisation of individual DT artefacts as a step towards compositional reasoning. We present a case study based on a DT-enabled greenhouse SoS modelled in VDM-RT (Vienna Development Method, Real-Time), including executable formal models, a property-based account of DT qualities, and an analysis of the obstacles arising when attempting to compose these artefacts at the SoS level. We consider how DT qualities may be operationalised as sets of verifiable properties. Formal modelling and analysis techniques and tools supporting their verification are identified. The study reveals how interpretations of DT qualities must adapt to different architectural roles and how local quality guarantees form assumptions for compositional reasoning. Full formalisation and verification of SoS composition is identified as future work.

</details>


### [260] [Rethinking Vulnerability Remediation as a Capacity Allocation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28509)
*Jana Stucke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究将漏洞修复重新定义为流量控制问题而非单一的优先级排序问题。通过分析Apache Jira、Mozilla Bugzilla、Red Hat安全勘误、五个公共Jira组织及npm依赖图，发现Apache修复时间呈重尾分布，94-100%的漏洞到达进入容量饱和的队列。队列上下文模型的预测判别力有限，而流量控制分析（队列状态转换、严重度优先排序、容量预留）显示出更大的操作效果，且所有者层面的容量只有在需求发生处或可通过专业知识连接转移时才有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI加速漏洞发现，修复吞吐量可能比优先级排序准确性更成为瓶颈。现有研究多聚焦于漏洞优先级排序（ranking），但忽略了修复流程的容量和流量控制问题。作者旨在论证将漏洞修复视为流量控制问题能带来更大的运营改进空间。

Method: 采用多源数据（Apache Jira、Mozilla Bugzilla、Red Hat errata、五个公共Jira组织、npm依赖图），从三个方面分析：(1) 队列建模与容量估计；(2) 队列上下文预测模型与简单基线对比；(3) 流量控制实验，包括队列状态转换效应、严重度优先排序策略和容量预留策略。

Result: Apache修复时间呈强重尾分布；94-100%的漏洞进入容量饱和队列；队列上下文模型预测力中等，与简单项目级基线相当；严重度优先排序可减少关键项延迟；容量预留可减少长期关键项延迟；容量仅在需求所在地或可通过专业知识连接转移时才有效。

Conclusion: 漏洞修复应被视为流量控制和容量分配问题，而非单纯的排序问题。关注容量瓶颈、队列动态和资源调配策略比仅优化优先级排序更能提升修复效率。

Abstract: As AI accelerates vulnerability discovery, remediation throughput may become a greater constraint than prioritisation accuracy. This study evaluates vulnerability remediation as a flow-control problem using Apache Jira, Mozilla Bugzilla, Red Hat security errata, five public Jira organisations, and an npm dependency graph. Apache resolution times are strongly heavy-tailed, while 94-100% of arrivals in the primary issue trackers enter queues estimated to be at or above capacity. Queue-context models provide only moderate predictive discrimination and are largely matched by simple project-level baselines. Severity-to-speed discrimination varies substantially across systems. Flow-control analyses show larger operational effects: transitions from overloaded to draining queues are associated with shorter resolution times, severity-first sequencing reduces critical-item delay at fixed capacity, and capacity reservation can reduce prolonged critical-item delays. Owner-level analyses further show that available capacity is useful only when it is located where demand occurs or can be transferred through relevant expertise connections. These findings support treating vulnerability remediation as a flow-control and capacity-allocation problem rather than solely a ranking problem.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Tensor-Accelerated Eager Multi-Resolution Grids for Evolving Large-Scale Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27612)
*Romain Claret,Michael O'Neill,Paul Cotofrei,Kilian Stoffel*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出EMR-HyperNEAT，通过将ES-HyperNEAT的顺序四叉树递归划分重构为"先全量评估、再按方差过滤"的并行化方案，解决了四叉树无法张量化的问题，在GPU上实现12-34倍加速并提升求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES-HyperNEAT的四叉树递归算法存在根本性局限：每层依赖于父节点的方差导致顺序执行，不同CPPN产生不同划分模式阻碍批处理，且可变叶子数量与JAX的静态形状要求不兼容。先前验证显示深度超过5时性能受限，JAX重实现仅获边际加速。

Method: EMR-HyperNEAT将ES-HyperNEAT的subdivide_if(var > θ)重构为eval_all() + filter(var > θ)两步：预先生成所有分辨率下所有位置的CPPN输出，再统一应用方差过滤。所有查询变得独立，可在核心间和种群成员间并行，将复杂度从O(4^D)降至O(4^D/P)。同时引入连接类型分类法实现循环基质配置。

Result: 在XOR基准上深度5-7下获得12-34倍的设备端GPU加速，并在多项基准上获得更高的求解率。

Conclusion: 通过牺牲部分冗余CPPN计算换取完全并行化，EMR-HyperNEAT打破了四叉树的顺序依赖瓶颈，使深层、高分辨率的神经进化在GPU上变得可行，同时提升了求解质量。

Abstract: In neuroevolution, indirect encoding generates neural network connectivity from a compact genome rather than specifying each connection. ES-HyperNEAT automatically discovers where to place hidden nodes by examining CPPN output patterns: it recursively subdivides space using a quadtree, expanding regions where CPPN outputs show high variance. This adaptive approach discovers network topology without manual substrate specification, extending the fixed-grid HyperNEAT framework built on NEAT.
  However, the quadtree resists tensorization. Each depth level depends on the parent's variance, forcing sequential evaluation. Different CPPNs produce different subdivision patterns, preventing batching. And variable leaf counts are incompatible with JAX's static shape requirement for JIT compilation. Our prior work confirmed these limits at depths exceeding 5, and a JAX reimplementation of the quadtree yielded only marginal speedup despite batched optimizations, motivating the eager reformulation presented here.
  We present EMR-HyperNEAT, which evaluates all positions at all resolutions up front, then filters using the same variance criterion: ES-HyperNEAT's subdivide_if(var > $θ$) becomes eval_all(); filter(var > $θ$). This performs more CPPN queries than necessary, but all queries become independent and parallelizable across both cores and population members, reducing complexity from \BigO($4^D$) to \BigO($4^D/P$) across $P$ parallel cores. Recurrent substrate configurations become feasible through a connection type taxonomy. The experiments section validates 12-34$\times$ on-device GPU speedup on XOR at depths 5-7, and empirically higher solve rates across benchmarks.

</details>

### [262] [Refundable Deposits: How to Restore Cooperation in Finitely Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27536)
*Giulio Salizzoni,Domenico Mergoni Cecchelli,Edward Plumb,Maryam Kamgarpour,Galit Ashkenazi-Golan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"押金"（deposits）的机制，通过在重复博弈中引入可退还押金来扩大有限重复博弈的纳什均衡集合，从而支持比标准均衡更有效率的收益配置，且不需要玩家之间的转移支付或事前承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限重复博弈拥有丰富的纳什均衡集合，但有限重复博弈的均衡往往更少且低效。作者希望在不改变底层博弈、不需要玩家承诺的情况下，通过外部中介的退款规则来扩大有限重复博弈的可实现均衡集合，从而支持更高效的合作结果。

Method: 在每一期中，玩家可自愿向中立中介支付可退还押金；游戏结束时押金返还，若发生偏离则押金被没收。押金支付是自愿且每阶段激励相容的，无需玩家承诺，仅需中介在博弈开始前承诺固定的退款规则。该机制通过构造新的子博弈精炼均衡来扩大均衡集合。

Result: 该机制能够支持比标准有限重复博弈均衡更有效的收益配置，并在囚徒困境、拥挤博弈（congestion game）和公共品博弈中证明了合作可以在标准有限重复版本中无法出现的情况下实现；同时将该构造扩展到动态公共池塘资源（common-pool resource）问题，表明方法可超越重复阶段博弈的框架。

Conclusion: 押金机制为有限重复博弈中的效率提升提供了一种新的途径：仅依靠中介的退款承诺和玩家的自愿押金，即可在不改变博弈结构、不引入玩家间转移支付的前提下扩大均衡集合并实现更高效率的合作结果。

Abstract: While infinitely repeated games admit a rich set of Nash equilibria, finitely repeated games typically have a much smaller and often inefficient one. We show how to enlarge this set using deposits: in each period a player may place a refundable sum with a neutral intermediary, returned when the game ends and forfeited following a deviation. Paying these deposits is voluntary and incentive compatible at every stage, so no commitment by the players is assumed, the only commitment required being that of the intermediary to a refund rule fixed before play begins. The mechanism sustains payoff profiles more efficient than those of the standard equilibria, without altering the underlying game and without transfers between players. We demonstrate it on the prisoner's dilemma, a congestion game, and a public goods game, all settings where cooperation cannot emerge in the standard finitely repeated version. We also apply it to a dynamic common-pool resource, suggesting that the construction extends beyond repeated stage-games.

</details>


### [263] [Approval-Based Apportionment: Like Portioning, Approximately like Committee Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27605)
*Paul Gölz,Hannane Yaghoubizade*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究批准制分配（approval-based apportionment）问题，即候选人（政党）可被多次选中的委员会选举变体。论文证明了多个比例性公理在分配设定下重合（EJR、EJR+、FJR 重合；PJR、PJR+、FPJR 重合），建立了 Lindahl 价格可支付性与 PAV 分数近似最优性的等价关系，并通过构造性方法将分配与比例分配（portioning）形式化连接，揭示分配比委员会选举更接近比例分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 委员会选举（每候选人0或1席）与比例分配（分数席位）之间缺乏系统的理论连接，而批准制分配（整数多席位）恰好位于两者之间，可能成为理解比例性公理关系的桥梁。作者希望借此发现新的公理蕴含关系，并推测委员会选举中近似公理关系。

Method: 1) 在分配设定下对多种比例性公理（EJR、EJR+、FJR、PJR、PJR+、FPJR）进行对比分析，证明其重合关系；2) 建立 Lindahl 价格可支付性（priceability）与 PAV 分数近似最优性之间的等价关系；3) 构造一个从分配公理到比例分配公理的"提升"（lifting）框架，在保持公理蕴含关系的前提下实现两种设定间的形式化连接；4) 证明局部 PAV 最优的委员会具有价格可支付性。

Result: 论文证明了：(1) EJR、EJR+、FJR 在分配设定下相互等价，PJR、PJR+、FPJR 也相互等价；(2) Lindahl 价格可支付性等价于 PAV 分数的一个近似最优性概念；(3) 局部 PAV 最优委员会是可价格支付的；(4) 通过提升构造，部分蕴含关系可扩展到比例分配，而部分仅近似成立。这些蕴含关系在委员会选举中均不成立。

Conclusion: 批准制分配在理论上更接近比例分配而非委员会选举。该设定为探索委员会选举中比例性公理之间的近似关系提供了肥沃土壤，且新的公理蕴含关系为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: We study approval-based apportionment, a variant of committee elections in which candidates ("parties") can be selected several times. We show that the proportionality axioms EJR, EJR+, and FJR coincide, and so do PJR, PJR+, and FPJR; that Lindahl priceability, an axiom implying core stability, is equivalent to a notion of approximate optimality with respect to the proportional approval voting (PAV) score; and that locally PAV-optimal committees are priceable.
  Approval-based apportionment (where a candidate receives an integer number of seats) lies between committee elections (zero or one seat) and portioning (a fractional number of seats). We formally connect portioning and apportionment by giving a construction that lifts axioms from apportionment to portioning and preserves implications between them. Several of our new implications between apportionment axioms are natural from a portioning perspective, leading us to believe that apportionment sits closer to portioning than to committee elections. None of them holds in committee elections, but several extend approximately, which makes apportionment a fruitful setting for conjecturing approximate relationships in approval-based committee elections.

</details>


### [264] [Condorcet-Winning Sets and Peer Selection in Planar Metric Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27653)
*Gabriel de Azevedo,Ulysse Hennebelle*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究平面度量选举中的Condorcet-winning集合问题。作者将一般度量选举、同行选择和一轮Voronoi博弈统一为两人Stackelberg博弈，并引入强同行选择的新变体。主要结果：在ℓ₁和ℓ∞范数下，每个平面实例都存在大小至多3的Condorcet-winning集合（通过强矩形ε-网新结果证明，改进Ashok等人的9/16阈值）；在ℓ₂范数下，每个平面度量选举存在大小至多4的Condorcet-winning集合（改进Song等人的上界5）；并给出了Voronoi博弈的与范数无关的新界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Condorcet-winning集合是排序投票中保证群体不被外部候选人多数偏好的候选人群组，具有重要的理论意义。已有研究给出了通用的上界（如Song等人的5），但针对平面度量选举这一特定且自然的模型，可以并值得获得更紧的界。此外，将多个看似不同的问题（度量选举、同行选择、Voronoi博弈）统一到共同的博弈论框架中，有助于揭示它们之间的深层结构联系。

Method: 作者将问题建模为两玩家Stackelberg博弈，建立统一层次框架。核心方法是通过强矩形ε-网的组合几何技巧：证明对平面中任意点集，可选择至多3个输入点与每个包含超过半数点的轴平行矩形相交。该结果直接导出ℓ₁和ℓ∞范数下Condorcet-winning集合的大小至多3。此外，通过不同的几何论证处理ℓ₂范数情形，得到大小至多4的界；对Voronoi博弈则给出独立于范数的通用界。

Result: (1) ℓ₁和ℓ∞范数下，平面度量选举（包括强同行选择）存在大小至多3的Condorcet-winning集合；(2) 强矩形ε-网新结果将阈值从9/16改进至1/2；(3) ℓ₂范数下，平面度量选举存在大小至多4的Condorcet-winning集合，改进Song等人的上界5；(4) 获得一轮Voronoi博弈的与范数无关的新上界。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入强矩形ε-网这一新的组合工具，显著改进了平面度量选举中Condorcet-winning集合的上界，并将多个相关问题统一在Stackelberg博弈框架下。这些结果展现了组合几何方法与社会选择理论交叉研究的价值，为后续在更高维空间或其他度量结构下的扩展研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In ranked-choice voting, a Condorcet-winning set is a group of candidates for which no outside candidate is preferred to every member of the group by a majority of voters. We study Condorcet-winning sets in planar metric elections, where voters rank candidates according to their distance under a given norm. We formulate general metric elections, peer selection, and the one-round Voronoi game as instances of a two-player Stackelberg game and place these problems in a common hierarchy. We also introduce a new variant, which we call strong peer selection.
  Our main result concerns peer selection under the $\ell_1$ and $\ell_\infty$ norms. We prove that every planar instance admits a Condorcet-winning set of size at most three, even under strong peer selection. This follows from a new result for strong rectangular $\varepsilon$-nets. We show that, for every set of points in the plane, one can choose at most three input points that intersect every axis-parallel rectangle containing more than half of the points, improving the previous threshold of $9 / 16$ due to Ashok et al. Under the $\ell_2$ norm, we prove that every planar metric election admits a Condorcet-winning set of size at most four, improving on the general bound of five due to Song et al. Finally, we give new norm-independent bounds for the one-round Voronoi game.

</details>


### [265] [Horizon-Independent Contraction for Continuous-Time Discounted Regularized Mean-Field Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27723)
*Junji Yan,Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了带折扣和熵正则化的非平稳连续时间平均场博弈（MFG）的收缩性质。在足够大的折扣率下，有限时域MFG存在与时间无关的收缩条件，且该条件与无限时域非平稳收缩条件一致。文章还推导了有限时域的精细收缩准则（基于谱半径），并给出了有限与无限时域均衡、折扣与非折扣均衡之间的显式误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间平均场博弈在折扣和熵正则化下的收缩性质尚未被充分理解。特别是，与无折扣情形相比，折扣情形下的有限时域收缩条件是否具有时间无关性，以及有限与无限时域均衡之间的关系，是值得研究的重要理论问题。

Method: 研究对象为状态和动作空间均有限的受控连续时间马尔可夫链。通过构造正向算子来刻画策略误差的传播，利用谱半径分析推导有限时域的精细收缩准则，并研究其在大时域极限下与无时域依赖收缩因子的关系。

Result: (1) 在足够大的折扣率下，有限时域MFG存在时域无关的收缩条件，与无限时域非平稳情形一致；(2) 获得有限与无限时域平均场均衡之间的显式收敛率；(3) 基于正算子谱半径推导出有限时域精细收缩准则，其大时域极限与无时域依赖收缩因子一致；(4) 给出折扣与非折扣有限时域正则化均衡之间的显式误差界。

Conclusion: 折扣率和熵正则化共同作用下，连续时间MFG在有限时域上展现出良好的收缩性质，且当折扣率足够大时，有限与无限时域的收缩行为趋于一致，为均衡的数值计算和理论分析提供了坚实的收缩性基础。

Abstract: We study contraction properties of non-stationary continuous-time mean-field games (MFGs) under discounting and entropy regularization. The state of the representative agent evolves according to a controlled continuous-time Markov chain, and both the state and action spaces are finite. In contrast to the undiscounted case, we show that, under a sufficiently large discount rate, finite-horizon MFGs admit a horizon-independent contraction condition, which also coincides with the corresponding infinite-horizon non-stationary contraction condition. As a byproduct, we obtain an explicit convergence rate between finite- and infinite-horizon mean-field equilibria. For each finite horizon, we further derive a refined contraction criterion from the spectral radius of a positive operator that majorizes the propagation of policy errors, and show that its large-horizon limit agrees with the horizon-independent contraction factor. Finally, we provide an explicit error bound between discounted and undiscounted finite-horizon regularized equilibria.

</details>


### [266] [Fine-Tuning Autobidders with Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28199)
*Anton Safin,Alexandra Khirianova,Andrey Pudovikov,Aleksandr Katrutsa,Egor Samosvat*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出将GRPO（组相对策略优化）框架从大语言模型后训练场景适配到自动出价（autobidding）问题中，替代传统actor-critic框架，以提高训练稳定性和鲁棒性。在BAT、iPinYou和AuctionNet等基准上实验表明，该方法在点击量上一致优于基线，在转化量上达到最优或次优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动出价是现代在线广告系统的核心组件，通常用actor-critic强化学习框架训练，但actor-critic的交替训练会导致不稳定且对噪声鲁棒性差。此外，自动出价场景与LLM后训练类似，最优出价事先未知，这为引入critic-free的策略梯度方法提供了契机。

Method: 将GRPO框架适配到自动出价场景：1) 采用无critic的策略梯度方法，避免actor-critic的交替训练不稳定问题；2) 将GRPO从LLM后训练迁移到出价生成函数训练中，用于增强强启发式基线的性能；3) 在自动出价中，动作是出价或相关的pace multiplier，值函数是给定出价下拍卖的期望回报。

Result: 在BAT、iPinYou和AuctionNet三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，Autobidding GRPO在点击量上持续优于所有基线（包括actor-critic模型、简单启发式和基于控制器的方法），在转化量上达到最佳或第二佳的表现。

Conclusion: GRPO框架可以有效迁移到自动出价场景，解决actor-critic训练不稳定和鲁棒性差的问题，且在多个广告基准上取得更优性能，为该领域提供了一种新的critic-free训练范式。

Abstract: Automated bidding (autobidding) is a core component of modern online advertising systems. Within this component, advertisers delegate sequential bid decisions to algorithms that must maximize campaign value while adhering to constraints such as a limited budget and a target cost-per-click (CPC). One of the approaches to resolve the autobidding problem is to formulate it as a Markov decision process and use reinforcement learning (RL) to train a bid generation function. The standard RL framework is actor-critic, which consists of an actor network that generates actions and a critic network that estimates the value of those actions. In our setting, the action is typically a bid or related pacing multiplier, and the value is the expected return from the auction given the bid. However, the alternating training of actor-critic RL models leads to instability and reduced robustness to noise. To address these issues, we adapt the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) framework to the autobidding setting. This framework is a \emph{critic-free} policy-gradient method originally developed for large language model post-training, where the ground-truth target is unknown. The autobidding setting shares this property, since the optimal bid is unknown in advance. Moreover, GRPO in the LLM domain is used to fine-tune the pre-trained model, and we use the same technique to enhance the performance of the strong heuristic baseline. We empirically compare Autobidding GRPO with actor-critic models, simple heuristics, and controller-based methods on the BAT, iPinYou, and AuctionNet benchmarks. Extensive experiments show that Autobidding GRPO consistently outperforms baselines in clicks and is the best or second-best method in conversion volume.

</details>


### [267] [A Constant Metric Distortion Protocol for Approval Voting Given Plurality Polls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28340)
*Fabian Frank,Jannik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仅有 plurality polls（即各候选人作为第一选择的选民比例）信息下如何改进 approval voting。作者提出一个参数化协议：每个选民批准其偏好列表中包含至少 k 比例选民第一选择的最短前缀。在最优 k 下，该规则达到 2+√5≈4.236 的 metric distortion，并证明 Bucklin 投票规则的 metric distortion 至多为 5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Approval voting 被广泛认可，但其一个主要局限是：当选民对候选人有潜在的严格偏好序时，不清楚应该批准哪些候选人。作者希望引入简单的 plurality polls 信息来解决这一模糊性，为 approval voting 提供基于信息的指导规则。

Method: 提出了一个以参数 k∈(0,1) 定义的简单协议：每个选民批准其偏好列表中包含至少 k 比例选民第一选择的最短前缀。通过 metric distortion 理论框架评估该协议，并分析了 Bucklin 投票规则的 distortion 上界，同时进行噪声鲁棒性分析。

Result: 该协议在最优 k 选择下实现 metric distortion 为 2+√5≈4.236；同时证明了 Bucklin 投票规则的 metric distortion 至多为 5；对所提协议的噪声鲁棒性进行了评估，并证明了其上界是紧的。

Conclusion: 作者证明了简单的 plurality poll 信息就能显著改善 approval voting 的效果，获得了常数级别的 metric distortion 保证，且该界限是紧的，表明该协议在理论上具有坚实的性能保障。

Abstract: Approval voting is a simple and well-regarded voting rule: voters submit approval ballots (subsets of the candidates) and the candidate receiving the most approvals wins. One major limitation of approval voting is that it is not clear which candidates voters should approve if they have an underlying strict order over the candidates. In this paper, we initiate the study of approval voting under a simple kind of information: plurality polls. That is, we assume that for each candidate we know the share of voters who rank this candidate as their top choice. Using these plurality polls, we suggest a simple protocol parameterized by a fraction $k \in (0,1)$: every voter should approve the smallest prefix of their preference list containing the first choices of at least a fraction $k$ of the voters. We evaluate this protocol via the framework of metric distortion and show that for the optimal choice of $k$, this rule achieves a metric distortion of $2 + \sqrt{5} \simeq 4.236$. The proof techniques we use for this statement also show that the Bucklin voting rule has a metric distortion of at most $5$. Finally, we evaluate the robustness of our protocol to noise and show that the upper bounds obtained for our protocol are tight.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [Marginal Coverage Credit Reduces Redundant Exploration in Parallel State-Entropy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27507)
*Junhao Cao,Hongyi Xia,Jianian Wu,Xiaopeng Yi,Lixia Huang,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCC-PGPSE方法，通过留一策略覆盖率和状态所有者专业化计算每个策略的边际覆盖贡献，将PGPSE的集体探索奖励重新分配给各策略，以鼓励互补覆盖而非冗余访问。在多个基准测试中取得了正向的归一化团队状态熵和状态支持度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGPSE虽然通过并行策略扩展状态空间覆盖，但其池化团队熵分数只能衡量集体探索程度，无法识别哪些策略贡献了非冗余的覆盖。这导致奖励分配缺乏策略级别的细粒度信号，难以促进策略间的互补覆盖。

Method: MCC-PGPSE结合两种机制计算策略特定信用：(1) leave-one-policy-out coverage（留一策略覆盖率），衡量移除某个策略后的覆盖损失；(2) state-owner specialization（状态所有者专业化），将状态归属到特定策略。方法保留PGPSE的池化目标，同时按照这些信用分数将非负辅助内在奖励重新分配，且不改变奖励总质量，从而抑制冗余访问并促进互补覆盖。

Result: 在受控环境、七个公开离散状态基准以及原始PGPSE协议的Room和Maze设置中，MCC-PGPSE在所有测试设置下均取得了正向的最终窗口增益（归一化团队状态熵和状态支持度相对于Entropy基线）。受控任务比较和固定套件聚合结果显著，五种子原始协议比较方向一致。消融和信用对齐控制表明大部分收益来自留一策略覆盖率而非非均匀加权、信用错配或神经新颖性。

Conclusion: 以贡献为条件的辅助奖励分配是一种可解释且有效的方法，能改善离散状态空间中并行策略之间的互补覆盖，为并行探索策略的信用分配问题提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Policy Gradient for Parallel State Entropy maximization (PGPSE) expands state-space coverage by training independently parameterized policies in replicated copies of the same environment. However, its pooled team-entropy score measures only collective exploration and cannot identify policies that contribute non-redundant coverage. We introduce Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE), which combines leave-one-policy-out coverage with state-owner specialization to estimate policy-specific credit. MCC-PGPSE preserves PGPSE's pooled objective and redistributes non-negative auxiliary intrinsic rewards according to these credits without changing their total mass. This redistribution is designed to discourage redundant visitation and promote complementary coverage. We evaluated MCC-PGPSE in controlled environments, seven public discrete-state benchmarks, and representative Room and Maze settings from the original PGPSE protocol. Across all tested settings, MCC-PGPSE produced positive final window gains in normalized team state entropy and state support over the Entropy baseline. Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [269] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了后训练量化（Post-training Quantization）常被错误地视为语义中立的优化过程，并形式化了"验证-部署鸿沟"（validation-deployment gap）：由于量化是参数空间上的多对一映射，源精度（全精度）检查并不能保证量化部署后的行为等价性。作者提出了量化行为等价类（QBECs）理论，证明QBEC成员资格不蕴含行为等价性，并以此为基础构建了三阶段对抗微调框架，可在通过源精度检查的模型中嵌入隐性恶意负载，在INT8或4-bit压缩后激活攻击行为。实验覆盖多语言编码器-解码器序列到序列模型的两个场景（战术机器翻译和政治内容分析），显示翻译模型在量化后友敌反转率从0上升至85.02%，立场分类器意识形态偏差ΔBias最高达0.33，且攻击在不同量化方案和架构间的持续性各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘AI部署流程中，后训练量化被视为语义中立的优化步骤，工作流上存在结构性缺陷：模型在全精度下完成评估和认证，量化在未经等效重新评估的情况下直接部署，导致源精度认证无法保证部署配置中的行为等价性。作者旨在形式化这一鸿沟，并证明其可被利用来实施量化触发的后门攻击，从而推动行为认证应覆盖最终部署配置这一安全理念。

Method: 1) 理论层面：形式化定义量化行为等价类（QBECs），证明QBEC成员资格不蕴含行为等价性，为量化触发后门攻击提供理论基础。2) 攻击层面：提出三阶段对抗微调框架，在全精度源检查通过的前提下，将隐性恶意负载嵌入模型，使其在INT8或4-bit压缩后激活目标对抗行为。3) 评估层面：将威胁场景从解码器-only因果LM扩展至多语言编码器-解码器序列到序列模型，评估战术机器翻译和政治内容分析两个操作场景，并进行跨量化器可迁移性分析。

Result: 1) 后门翻译模型在修复FP16下友敌破坏率为零，量化后反转率最高达85.02%；2) 成对立场分类器在压缩后意识形态偏移达ΔBias=0.33；3) 跨量化器可迁移性分析显示攻击持续性由量化方案和模型架构共同决定，而非仅由名义位宽决定；4) 攻击可跨模型类型（从因果LM迁移到序列到序列模型）复现。

Conclusion: 源精度审计无法排除量化触发行为，最终部署配置必须纳入行为认证范围，才能实现可信的边缘AI。量化不是语义中立的过程，其引入的行为变化可能被恶意利用，因此部署层面的安全验证不可或缺。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [270] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究基于Gated Delta Net和Kimi Delta Attention的语言模型中循环状态的后训练量化问题，提出DAMP方法，通过离线校准识别高风险的通道并以更高精度存储，其余采用INT8。在9.9 bits/state下保持接近FP32精度，存储减少69.1%，加速2.01倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDN和KDA模型用固定大小的循环状态替代KV cache以降低推理内存，但这些状态以FP32存储，消耗大量GPU内存且更新受限于内存带宽，显著增加解码延迟。已有均匀量化方案（INT8/FP8/INT4/NVFP4）精度-存储权衡不佳，需要更优的量化方案。

Method: DAMP方法：基于两个发现——（1）多数量化误差能量集中在少部分通道；（2）状态通道的相对衰减强度在跨提示和任务间保持稳定。利用量化误差能量和基于衰减的持久性，在离线校准阶段识别高风险通道，以更高精度存储这些通道，其余通道用INT8存储。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B上，覆盖数学推理、通用推理和代码生成六个基准的平均精度接近FP32基线。循环状态存储减少69.1%，循环状态更新内核加速最高2.01倍，全模型TPOT降低最高10.9%。

Conclusion: DAMP通过混合精度量化策略有效解决了循环状态的内存和延迟瓶颈，在不明显损失精度的前提下大幅降低了存储并加速推理，为GDN/KDA类模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Softmax attention stores key and value vectors for every preceding token, causing inference memory to grow with sequence length. Recent language models incorporating Gated DeltaNet (GDN) or Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) reduce this cost by replacing the KV cache in most layers with fixed-size recurrent states. However, these recurrent states are commonly stored in FP32 and consume substantial GPU memory; their updates are memory-bandwidth bound and contribute significantly to decoding latency. To our knowledge, we are the first to study post-training quantization of recurrent states in GDN and KDA based language models. We find that uniform quantization provides a poor accuracy--storage trade-off: INT8 and FP8 already degrade accuracy on complex reasoning tasks, while INT4 and NVFP4 reduce it to near zero. We further find that most quantization-error energy is concentrated in a small subset of channels and that the relative decay strength of state channels remains stable across prompts and tasks. Motivated by these findings, DAMP uses both quantization-error energy and decay-based persistence to identify high-risk channels during offline calibration. It stores these channels at higher precision and the remainder in INT8. We evaluate DAMP on Qwen3.6-35B and Kimi-Linear-48B across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation. At 9.9 bits per state value, DAMP maintains average accuracy close to the FP32 baseline. DAMP reduces recurrent-state storage by 69.1%, accelerates the recurrent-state update kernel by up to 2.01x, and lowers full-model TPOT by up to 10.9%.

</details>


### [271] [A Deeper Analysis of Block-Sparse Featurizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27515)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea,Amith Ananthram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了块稀疏特征化器（BSF）的优缺点，发现其仍存在经典SAE的失败模式（特征分裂和组合），提出包括Tournament Top-K选择规则在内的多项架构改进以减少特征分裂，并将块范式扩展到交叉编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSF作为SAE的变体，以子空间（块）为原子单元，旨在捕捉视觉中常见的低维流形特征，但其性能尚未被充分研究，且仍受经典SAE失败模式（特征分裂、组合）影响。

Method: 系统分析BSF的优缺点；提出架构改进，包括Tournament Top-K选择规则（显著减少特征分裂）；将块范式扩展到交叉编码器（crosscoder）。

Result: Tournament Top-K选择规则显著减少了特征分裂问题；块范式成功扩展到交叉编码器。

Conclusion: BSF虽有优势但仍继承SAE的部分缺陷，通过架构改进（如Tournament Top-K）可有效缓解，且块范式具有跨架构的泛化潜力。

Abstract: The recently introduced block-sparse featurizer (BSF; Fel et al., 2026) is similar to a sparse autoencoder (SAE), but its atomic unit is a small subspace (a block of directions) rather than a single direction. It is designed for features that live on low-dimensional manifolds, which are especially frequent in vision. This work studies the BSF's strengths and weaknesses, finding how it still somewhat suffers from classic SAE failure modes, like feature splitting and composition. We propose several architectural changes to the BSF, including a Tournament Top-K selection rule that significantly reduces feature splitting, and we also extend the block paradigm to the crosscoder.

</details>


### [272] [When Muon Meets Task Interference: A Spectral Perspective on Continual Learning and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27518)
*Shangge Liu,Yuehan Yin,Yinghuan Shi,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现持续学习（CL）中的灾难性遗忘和模型合并（MM）中的权重解纠缠误差本质上是同一现象——"任务干扰"（task interference），即一个任务的参数更新会改变模型在另一个任务上的输出。作者将此形式化为层间Frobenius内积，并证明优化器通过谱范数‖ΔW‖₂控制这一干扰。Muon优化器因其内在的谱范数调控机制，能同时改善CL和MM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将CL和MM中的困难视为独立问题进行分别处理，忽略了基础优化器诱导的几何结构对这两类问题的影响。作者希望揭示这两个看似不同的问题背后的统一机制，并利用优化器本身的特性来缓解它们。

Method: 1) 将CL和MM的困难统一形式化为"任务干扰"，用层间Frobenius内积⟨ΔWℓ, Jℓ(x)⟩F度量。2) 理论上推导出干扰上界，隔离出优化器可控制的谱范数因子‖ΔWℓ‖₂。3) 进行逐模式（per-mode）分析验证理论上界能捕捉经验干扰的主要部分。4) 识别Muon优化器通过构造性设计调控谱范数，从而收紧干扰界。

Result: 1) 在八任务模型合并基准上（三个CLIP主干），用Muon替代AdamW可获得最高+5.02的精度提升。2) 在持续学习的十个类增量协议、三个任务增量协议和11任务MTIL基准上，Muon均带来一致的正面收益。

Conclusion: CL和MM的核心困难统一为任务干扰，且优化器通过谱范数发挥着关键作用。Muon作为优化器层面的原则性方案，能与现有解决方案互补，为CL和MM提供统一的优化器中心视角。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) and model merging (MM) both aim to obtain a single model that performs well across multiple tasks, challenged respectively by catastrophic forgetting and weight-disentanglement error.
